On benchmarks that grade against every detail of the context, even strong open-weights models pass only 12-16% of tasks: a single overlooked rule fails the whole response. We argue this brittleness is structural: the dominant "read-and-reason" paradigm asks the model to extract, plan, generate, and self-verify in one forward pass. We therefore ask whether explicit context compilation can fix it, how it compares to existing long-context strategies (gist retrieval, multi-agent self-play), and where the resulting harness benefit holds across task structure and model scale. We propose the Context Compilation Architecture (CCA), whose central novelty is a typed intermediate representation (IR) with fixed slots (rules.{must_do, must_not, conditional}, output_spec, available_tools, data_profile) into which any prose context is compiled once; executable verifiers and a violation-gated correction loop follow as downstream consequences. On CL-bench (1,899 tasks across 4 open base models), CCA outperforms vanilla prompting and two long-context baselines (ReadAgent-P, Ctx2Skill) on every base model, lifting Kimi K2.5 from 15.4% to 21.4% with gains concentrated on rule-dense sub-categories. Code and cached completions are available at https://github.com/TonyQJH/cca-emnlp2026.

</details>


### [147] [A Unified Mechanistic Analysis of Knowledge- and Safety-Based Refusals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00760)
*Yuri Son,Seunghee Kim,Hyuhng Joon Kim,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过213个对比四元组数据集，系统研究了LLM中基于知识的拒绝（KR）和基于安全的拒绝（SR）在机制上的异同，发现两者共享拒绝方向但机制不对称，表现为"先承诺后细化"的分层处理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越常被训练来拒绝两类查询：超出其知识范围的查询（知识型拒绝，KR）和违反安全策略的查询（安全型拒绝，SR）。尽管两者在表面响应上相似，但此前研究通常将它们分开处理，对于它们是否共享底层机制这一问题缺乏系统性探究。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含213个对比四元组的新数据集，联合探测两种拒绝类型，通过表征分析等方法系统研究KR与SR在模型内部机制上的异同。

Result: 发现KR和SR受控于重叠但可区分的机制：两者共享一个拒绝方向，但重叠是不对称的——SR信号向KR的迁移强于反向迁移。类型特异的专业化主要出现在模型上层，KR与不确定性和知识相关表征对齐，SR与安全和策略相关表征对齐。

Conclusion: 将拒绝机制刻画为"先承诺后细化"（commit-then-specify）过程：共享的初始机制先做出拒绝承诺，随后在更高层中类型特异的特征决定拒绝的依据是基于认识论（知识）还是规范性（安全）的。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained to decline queries that fall outside their knowledge (knowledge-based refusal, KR) or violate safety policies (safety-based refusal, SR). Although KR and SR result in superficially similar responses, they have largely been studied in isolation, leaving open whether they share an underlying mechanism. We address this gap with a systematic study on a new dataset of 213 contrastive quadruples that jointly probe both refusal types. We find that KR and SR are governed by overlapping yet distinguishable mechanisms. Both share a refusal direction, yet the overlap is asymmetric: SR signals transfer more strongly to KR than the reverse. Type-specific specialization emerges mainly in upper layers, with KR aligning with uncertainty- and knowledge-related representations and SR with safety- and policy-related ones. We thus characterize refusal as a commit-then-specify process: a shared initial mechanism commits to refusing, then type-specific features in later layers specify whether the grounds are epistemic or normative.

</details>


### [148] [Instella-MoE Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00791)
*Jiang Liu,Sudhanshu Ranjan,Prakamya Mishra,Yonatan Dukler,Gowtham Ramesh,Jialian Wu,Ximeng Sun,Wen Xie,Chaojun Hou,Vikram Appia,Zhenyu Gu,Zicheng Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Instella-MoE，一个完全开源的混合专家（MoE）语言模型，拥有160亿总参数和28亿活跃参数（每token），完全从头开始在AMD Instinct MI300X和MI325X GPU上训练。模型在预训练基准上平均得分76.7，超越OLMo-3-7B、SmolLM3-3B和OLMoE-1B-7B等完全开源模型，并与Moonlight-16B-A3B和Qwen3.5-4B等开源权重MoE/密集模型相当。后训练后，Think检查点在指令跟随、推理、数学、编码和聊天基准上平均得分73.2，超越同类或更大活跃参数的模型。作者发布了完整的模型流程，包括权重、训练配置、数据混合和训练代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型领域缺乏完全开源（不仅开放权重，还开放训练流程和数据）的高效MoE模型。现有开源MoE模型在性能或透明度上存在局限，作者旨在构建一个既高效又完全透明可复现的MoE基础模型，以推动可重复研究和社区发展。

Method: 采用稀疏激活的MoE架构（160亿总参数/28亿活跃参数），融合了Gated Multi-head Latent Attention (Gated MLA)和FarSkip-Collective连接等架构与系统级创新。训练采用多阶段流水线，包括预训练、中期训练、长上下文扩展、基于反馈驱动数据策展的监督微调、直接偏好优化（DPO）以及基于多教师策略蒸馏的强化学习。全部训练在AMD Instinct MI300X和MI325X GPU上进行。

Result: 预训练基准平均得分76.7，超越OLMo-3-7B、SmolLM3-3B和OLMoE-1B-7B等完全开源模型；与Moonlight-16B-A3B和Qwen3.5-4B等规模相当的开源权重模型保持竞争力。后训练的Think检查点在指令跟随、推理、数学、编码和聊天基准上平均得分73.2，超越可比或更大活跃参数的模型。

Conclusion: Instella-MoE为高效、高性能的完全开源MoE模型和可复现研究奠定了坚实基础。通过发布完整的模型流程（权重、训练配置、数据混合和训练代码），该工作显著提升了MoE大模型研究的透明度和可复现性。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Instella-MoE, a fully open Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model with 16 billion total parameters and 2.8 billion active parameters per token, trained entirely from scratch on AMD Instinct MI300X and MI325X GPUs. Instella-MoE combines a sparsely activated MoE design with architectural and system-level innovations, including Gated Multi-head Latent Attention (Gated MLA) and FarSkip-Collective connectivity, enabling efficient large-scale training and inference. The model is developed through a multi-stage pipeline comprising pre-training, mid-training, long-context extension, supervised fine-tuning with feedback-driven data curation, direct preference optimization, and reinforcement learning with Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation. Instella-MoE achieves an average score of 76.7 across standard pre-training benchmarks, outperforming prior fully open models including OLMo-3-7B, SmolLM3-3B, and OLMoE-1B-7B, while remaining competitive with open-weight MoE and dense baselines at comparable active-parameter scales, including Moonlight-16B-A3B and Qwen3.5-4B. After post-training, our final Think checkpoint achieves an average score of 73.2 across instruction-following, reasoning, math, coding, and chat benchmarks, outperforming both fully open and open-weight models with comparable or larger active parameter counts in our evaluation. To support transparent and reproducible research, we release the complete Instella-MoE model flow, including model weights, training configurations, data mixtures, and training code. Together, these contributions establish Instella-MoE a strong, fully open foundation for efficient, high-performing MoE models and reproducible research.

</details>


### [149] [SFAD: Speculative Factuality-Aware Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00796)
*Guanqiao Chen,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SFAD是一个投机性解码框架，通过构建ConFide偏好数据集训练上下文忠实的草稿模型，并结合Epistemic Friction检测幻觉与Asymmetric Logit Steering精炼目标分布，在不降低推理效率的前提下提升大语言模型的上下文忠实度，实现了2.48倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在知识密集型应用中的上下文忠实度至关重要，但现有方法存在显著缺陷：对比解码需要双重前向传播导致推理开销翻倍；后训练对齐需要大量强化学习和计算成本。如何在保证上下文忠实度的同时不牺牲生成效率是核心挑战。

Method: SFAD框架包含三个关键组件：(1)构建ConFide，一个带有细粒度原子扰动的偏好数据集，通过DPO训练上下文忠实的草稿模型；(2)推理阶段引入Epistemic Friction机制，通过量化由专家确定性加权的分布张力来检测潜在幻觉；(3)当检测到越界时，采用非对称对数几率引导（Asymmetric Logit Steering）通过基于残差的对数几率注入精炼目标分布；否则执行标准投机解码流程。

Result: 大量实验表明，SFAD在显著提升上下文忠实度的同时实现了2.48倍的推理加速，证明了该框架在高效大语言模型中具有实际可行性。

Conclusion: SFAD提供了一种兼顾效率与忠实度的实用方案，在投机解码框架内同时实现了幻觉抑制和推理加速，为知识密集型应用中高效可靠的LLM部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: As one of the most critical challenges in large language models, contextual faithfulness directly determines their reliability in knowledge-intensive applications. This task is particularly challenging as it requires balancing factual consistency with generation efficiency. Contrastive decoding methods require dual forward passes (with and without context) to compare model outputs, doubling inference computational overhead, while post-training alignment demands extensive reinforcement learning with substantial computational overhead. To address this challenge, we present \textbf{SFAD}, a speculative decoding framework that enhances contextual faithfulness without inference degradation. We first construct \textbf{ConFide}, a preference dataset with fine-grained atomic perturbations, to train a context-faithful draft model via Direct Preference Optimization. During inference, Epistemic Friction detects potential hallucinations by quantifying distributional tension weighted by specialist certainty. When friction exceeds the threshold, Asymmetric Logit Steering refines the target distribution through residual-based logit injection; otherwise, standard speculation proceeds. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SFAD substantially improves faithfulness while achieving $2.48\times$ speedup, offering a practical solution for efficient LLMs.

</details>


### [150] [TEIDAN: A Multilingual Multiparty Dialogue Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00802)
*Taiga Mori,Koji Inoue,Mikey Elmers,Divesh Lala,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TEIDAN，一个多语种多模态语料库，涵盖日语和英语的三方自发面对面对话，记录开放话题讨论并基于IPU提供转写文本，用于支持人机交互与人类对话研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人互动是人类沟通的核心场景，也是参与群体对话的人机交互系统必须处理的目标。然而现有语料库多聚焦于会议、任务导向交互、文本交互或表演式场景，缺乏支持跨语言比较的自发面对面三方讨论语料资源。

Method: TEIDAN语料库采集日本和英语两组三方参与者进行开放式话题讨论，每位参与者配备独立针式麦克风，同时使用麦克风阵列和面向参与者的摄像头进行录制，并基于IPU（inter-pausal unit）为两种语言部分提供转写文本，未来计划扩展至更多语言。

Result: 以往研究仅使用日语部分子集用于多方言对话建模的任务特定基准；本文则完整呈现TEIDAN作为涵盖日语和英语的语料库资源，提供采集设计、参与者信息、录制设置、转写格式及语料统计，并通过初步分析展示其在话轮转换、受话人识别和多模态语境理解方面的应用潜力。

Conclusion: TEIDAN为跨语言的自发多方面对面对话研究提供了稀缺的语料资源，可支撑人类与人类、人类与智能体交互中的话轮转换、受话人识别和多模态语料研究，并有进一步扩展多语言的计划。

Abstract: Multi-party interaction is a central setting for human communication and a necessary target for human-agent interaction systems that must participate in group conversation. Yet available corpora often focus on meetings, task-oriented interaction, text-based interaction, or acted scenarios, and fewer resources support cross-linguistic comparison of spontaneous face-to-face triadic discussion. This paper presents TEIDAN, a multilingual multimodal corpus that currently consists of Japanese and English three-party conversations. TEIDAN records groups of three participants discussing open-ended topics with individual pin microphones, a microphone array, and participant-facing cameras, and provides IPU-based transcripts for both language portions. Earlier studies used subsets of the Japanese portion for task-specific benchmarks in multi-party dialogue modeling; in contrast, this paper presents TEIDAN as a corpus resource spanning both Japanese and English, with planned expansion to additional languages. We describe the collection design, participants, recording setup, transcription format, and corpus statistics, and provide preliminary analyses to illustrate how TEIDAN can support research on turn-taking, addressee recognition, and multimodal grounding in human-human and human-agent interaction.

</details>


### [151] [TWIX: a Two-Stage Approach for End-To-End Named Entity Recognition and Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00832)
*Marco Martinelli,Laura Menotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TWIX，一个用于肠-脑轴领域信息抽取的两阶段端到端管道，在GutBrainIE基准上显著超越基线，并在所有子任务中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献的指数级增长迫切需要自动化的信息抽取系统来支持知识发现，而肠-脑轴领域缺乏统一的IE基准与高性能解决方案。

Method: 提出TWIX（Two-stage Workflow for Information eXtraction），一种端到端IE管道，由三个相互关联的模块组成，每个模块均采用两阶段框架，用于解决GutBrainIE基准中的全部四个子任务（NER、NERD和RE等）。

Result: 在开发集和测试集上的评估表明，TWIX大幅超越基线性能，并在所有子任务的所有参赛提交中排名第一，有效提升了精确率和召回率。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段管道在实际场景中能有效改进信息抽取的精确率和召回率，验证了该方法在肠-脑轴领域知识发现中的实用价值。

Abstract: The exponential growth of scientific publications calls for automatic Information Extraction (IE) systems to support knowledge discovery. In this context, the GutBrainIE benchmark evaluates Named Entity Recognition (NER), Named Entity Recognition and Disambiguation (NERD), and Relation Extraction (RE) systems in the gut-brain axis domain. We propose Two-stage Workflow for Information eXtraction (TWIX), an end-to-end IE pipeline featuring three interconnected modules, each leveraging a two-stage framework to solve all four GutBrainIE subtasks. Evaluation on the development and test sets shows that our method substantially outperforms the baseline by a wide margin, while also ranking first among all participant submissions across all subtasks. These results indicate that the proposed two-stage pipeline effectively improves both precision and recall in practical settings.

</details>


### [152] [Dense Process Supervision for Search Agents via Fact Utility Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00833)
*Rongzhi Zhu,Xiangyu Liu,Yi Liu,Shuo Zhang,Ruirui Zhang,Rui Wu,Tao Jiang,Zequn Sun,Wenhao Xu,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于事实效用估计的稠密过程监督方法，用于搜索代理的强化学习，在多个QA基准上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索代理的强化学习通常依赖结果奖励，但结果奖励无法有效进行信用分配，因为中间步骤的价值难以从最终结果中分离出来，导致难以判断每个中间步骤的贡献。

Method: 将推理过程建模为离散证据事实的累积。首先从原始观测中提取结构化事实并组织成显式事实存储；然后对语义等价的事实进行聚类，并通过贝叶斯估计在组rollout上推断每个事实聚类的后验效用；最后将估计的事实效用转换为稠密的步级奖励以指导RL训练。

Result: 在七个单跳和多跳QA基准上，该方法一致优于现有基线。消融研究表明，与仅使用结果奖励训练相比，在多跳QA上取得了明确的相对改进。

Conclusion: 基于事实效用的稠密过程监督能有效改善搜索代理的信用分配问题，尤其对多跳推理任务提升显著。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for search agents typically relies on outcome rewards. However, it often fails to achieve effective credit assignment, due to the unclear value of intermediate steps. It is hard to separate their contributions from the final result. In this paper, we propose a dense process supervision method based on fact utility estimation, which models the reasoning process as the accumulation of discrete evidence facts. We first extract structured facts from raw observations and organize them into an explicit fact store. To support credit assignment, we then cluster semantically equivalent facts and infer the posterior utility of each fact cluster using Bayesian estimation over group rollouts. Finally, we convert the estimated fact utilities into dense step-level rewards to guide RL training. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms existing baselines. Ablation studies validate clear relative improvements on multi-hop QA compared to outcome reward-only training.

</details>


### [153] [Staged Linguistic Seeding: Grounded Query Expansion for Verified-Unit QA in AI Contact Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00844)
*Hyeonseop Yoon,Jeong-Eun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种面向AI呼叫中心客服QA的部署系统，该系统仅从经过验证的封闭QA单元集合中作答，通过离线"分级语言播种"(SLS)方法增强检索索引——人工编写每个QA单元的槽位配方，gpt-4.1-mini生成变体并经轻量人工门控筛选。在两个工业领域上，SLS将混合R@1提升至0.881/0.930（+0.27/+0.34），优于doc2query基线，并将自由生成式回答的不受支持内容率从7-13%降至约0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户服务QA在AI呼叫中心(AICC)中面临严格的部署约束：语音热线延迟要求苛刻，且错误或不受支持的自由生成式回答代价高昂。现有QA基准和检索增强生成方案无法满足这些约束下的可靠性与延迟需求，需要一种仅从封闭验证集作答的部署方案。

Method: 提出分级语言播种(SLS)方法：离线阶段由人类为每个QA单元撰写基于世界知识锚定的槽位配方(slot recipe)，使用gpt-4.1-mini将配方渲染为多样化查询变体，再经轻量人工门控过滤。推理阶段保持仅有单次检索pass、无查询时生成的架构，确保延迟可控。同一方法论跨两个领域复用。系统支持检索命中即原文作答、不明确时澄清、无法回答时放弃或转人工等路由策略。

Result: 在来自两个工业领域的保留查询变体上，SLS将混合R@1提升至0.881/0.930（相较基线+0.27/+0.34），且在全部五个测试检索器上均获得增益。在相同gpt-4.1-mini生成预算下，SLS比doc2query高出+0.20/+0.32。跨来源评估提供迁移性证据。验证单元作答将不受支持内容率从自由生成的7-13%降至约0%。

Conclusion: 验证单元作答配合SLS检索增强是在AICC语音热线约束下兼顾准确性与低延迟的可行方案，能显著消除自由生成的不受支持内容风险。论文作为应用研究发布，并包含负结果报告，表明该方法具有跨域可复用性与实际部署价值。

Abstract: Customer-service QA in an AI contact center (AICC) runs under deployment constraints that benchmark QA misses: tight voice-hotline latency and a high cost for unsupported or wrong automatic answers. We deploy a system that answers only from a closed set of verified QA units: it returns a retrieved unit verbatim, or routes to clarify, abstain, or handoff. The index is enriched offline by staged linguistic seeding (SLS): a human authors a per-unit world-grounded slot recipe, gpt-4.1-mini renders it into variants, and a light human gate filters them. One methodology is reused across both domains, so inference stays a single retrieval pass with no query-time generation. On held-out query variants from two industrial domains, SLS lifts hybrid R@1 to 0.881/0.930 (+0.27/+0.34), with gains across all five retrievers tested. At the same gpt-4.1-mini generation budget, SLS beats doc2query by +0.20/+0.32, while cross-provenance evaluation provides additional evidence of transfer across generated-query distributions. Verified-unit answering also removes free-form generation's unsupported-content surface (7-13% versus approximately 0%). We report this as an application study, including negative results.

</details>


### [154] [Membership Inference in Fine-tuned Diffusion Language Models via Token-level Memorization Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00873)
*Shengfang Zhai,Leo Marchyok,Yuling Shi,Huanran Chen,Yinpeng Dong,Jiaheng Zhang,Sanghyun Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别了扩散语言模型中一种名为"token级记忆不对称性"的现象，并提出基于分位数加权偏度的Q-Skew指标，用于对微调后的DLM进行成员推断攻击，实验表明该方法优于现有基线，且能扩展到PII提取等其他隐私攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）作为自回归LM的替代范式日益受到关注，但其隐私风险尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，通过理论分析扩散训练动态来理解并检测DLM中的隐私泄露。

Method: 首先通过理论分析扩散训练动力学，识别出"token级记忆不对称性"现象；基于此发现，提出Q-Skew——一种基于分位数加权偏度的指标，用于对微调后的扩散语言模型进行成员推断。

Result: 在多个微调数据集和模型上，Q-Skew优于现有基线方法；此外，Q-Skew还可用于促进PII提取等其他隐私违规攻击。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了此前未被充分探索的DLM隐私攻击面，强调了对DLM进行系统性隐私评估的必要性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently emerged as an alternative modeling paradigm to autoregressive LMs, offering advantages such as parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling. Despite growing interest in their generative capabilities, the privacy risks of DLMs remain underexplored. We identify a phenomenon termed token-level memorization asymmetry through theoretical analysis of diffusion training dynamics. Building on this finding, we propose Q-Skew, a quantile-weighted skewness-based indicator for membership inference on finetuned DLMs. Experiments across multiple fine-tuning datasets and models show that our method outperforms existing baselines. Moreover, we show that Q-Skew can also facilitate other privacy violations, such as PII extraction. Our findings reveal a previously underexplored privacy attack surface and highlight the need for systematic privacy evaluation of DLMs.

</details>


### [155] [Context-Grounding Gains Are Mediated by Pre-existing Machinery: Auditing GRPO, SFT, and DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00925)
*Prakhar Gupta,Vaibhav Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了九种后训练方法（GRPO、SFT、DPO）对语言模型"遵循提示证据胜过记忆知识"（grounding）能力的影响。结果发现：GRPO系列变体的grounding增益很小；冲突SFT适度提升grounding；DPO在其匹配分布上几乎将grounding推向天花板。更重要的是，这些增益主要依赖于起始模型中已存在的机制（因果注意力头），而非后训练引入的全新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练能让语言模型更可靠地遵循提示中的证据，但尚不清楚这些能力提升是源于真正的新机制，还是仅仅强化了模型中已有的机制。厘清这一问题有助于理解模型行为变化的本质来源。

Method: 从同一初始检查点出发，比较9种后训练方案（涵盖GRPO、SFT、DPO三个家族），并在不同尺度和模型家族间扩展关键对比。训练前从模型估计一个"grounding方向"（grounding direction）。测试5种GRPO变体，对两种做跨种子等效性检验。此外还包含消融实验：减去起始模型的grounding方向、将起始模型方向叠加回模型，以及监督热身（supervised warm start）后施加GRPO等操作。

Result: （1）5种GRPO变体的grounding增益均很小，跨种子复制时其效应被等效性检验界定在冲突SFT增益之下；（2）冲突SFT适度提升grounding；（3）DPO几乎使grounding达到天花板；（4）冲突SFT和DPO使用的因果注意力头集合与起始模型基本一致；（5）减去起始模型方向会抑制两类增益，而将其加回起始模型可恢复DPO增益的35%；（6）监督热身后再用相同GRPO几乎无额外增益。

Conclusion: 在本文设定下，grounding能力的提升主要依赖于起始检查点中已存在的基础机制，而非后训练创造的新机制。

Abstract: Language models can ignore prompt evidence when it conflicts with memorized knowledge. Post-training can make models follow such evidence more reliably, but it is unclear whether these gains require new machinery or strengthen machinery already present. We compare nine post-training arms spanning GRPO, SFT, and DPO from one starting checkpoint, with key comparisons extended across scales and families. We estimate a grounding direction from that checkpoint before training. Across five tested GRPO variants, grounding gains are small. For the two variants replicated across seeds, equivalence tests bound their effects below the conflict-SFT gain even as the rewarded metric improves. Conflict-SFT improves grounding moderately, while DPO drives grounding near ceiling on its matched distribution. Conflict-SFT and DPO largely use the same causal attention-head set as the starting model. Subtracting the starting-model direction suppresses both gains, while adding it to the starting model recovers 35% of DPO's gain at a dose passing all stated side-effect checks. After a supervised warm start makes the context answer appear in more rollouts, the same GRPO recipe adds essentially no further grounding gain. In our setting, grounding gains largely depend on machinery already present in the starting model.

</details>


### [156] [DualStake: Dual-Path Confidence Calibration in Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00935)
*Yinuo Xu,Yuwei Liang,Jianjie Cheng,Meng Wang,Yongcan Yu,Shuo Lu,Jian Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DualStake方法，通过在Deep Research智能体检索步骤后引入证据置信度（E-Conf）elicitation，并采用双路径校准方法（margin-clipped、置信度依赖的stake奖励），联合对齐证据置信度和答案置信度与答案正确性，从而解决深度研究智能体在不确定性量化上的过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep Research智能体在执行知识密集型任务时存在严重的过度自信问题，其表达出的置信度不可靠，导致用户信任和下游拒答（abstention）决策受损。现有的后答案口头化置信度（verbalized confidence）信号不足以提供可靠的不确定性估计。

Method: 1) 在每次检索步骤后引入步骤级置信度elicitation（Step Confidence Elicitation），补充常用的答案后口头化置信度；2) 发现最终检索步骤后的证据置信度（E-Conf）比答案生成后的答案置信度（A-Conf）提供更强的不确定性信号，且A-Conf在很大程度上由E-Conf塑形；3) 提出DualStake双路径校准方法，应用margin-clipped、置信度依赖的stake奖励，联合校准E-Conf和A-Conf与答案正确性的对齐，同时限制极端置信度优化。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B三个模型上，基于8个QA基准数据集的实验表明，DualStake持续提升校准性能且不牺牲答案准确性。

Conclusion: 证据置信度（E-Conf）是比答案置信度（A-Conf）更强的不确定性信号，DualStake通过双路径校准机制有效缓解了Deep Research智能体的过度自信问题，在提升置信度可靠性的同时保持答案质量。

Abstract: Deep Research agents tackle knowledge-intensive tasks through multi-round retrieval and decision-oriented generation. However, these agents suffer from severe overconfidence, making their expressed confidence unreliable for user trust and downstream abstention. To address this, we augment the Deep Research pipeline with step confidence elicitation after each retrieval, building on the commonly used post-answer verbalized confidence. Interestingly, we find that Evidence Confidence (E-Conf), elicited after the final retrieval step, provides a stronger uncertainty signal than Answer Confidence (A-Conf), elicited after answer generation, and that A-Conf is largely shaped by E-Conf. Based on these findings, we propose DualStake, a dual-path calibration method that applies margin-clipped, confidence-dependent stake rewards to jointly align E-Conf and A-Conf with answer correctness while limiting extreme confidence optimization. Experiments on Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Qwen3-4B across 8 QA benchmarks demonstrate that DualStake consistently improves calibration without sacrificing answer accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/FloXXXt/DualStake.

</details>


### [157] [A Dataset for Modeling Iterative Problem-Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00940)
*Fagun Patel,Sang T. Truong,Duc Q. Nguyen,Kazunori Fukuhara,Benjamin W. Domingue,Sanmi Koyejo,Nick Haber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeInsight，一个包含3286名本科生在两个学年两门C++入门课程中超过300万次编程提交的大规模数据集，并在此基础上构建了一个统一校准与评分协议下的基准，评估参数化、序列和生成三类模型。作者将循环状态空间模型（RSSM）适配为通过离散潜变量追踪求解者特征，在四个课程中的三个上取得了最强的预测准确率；LLM预测器虽准确率较低但能生成完整提交，作者发现LLM的编码能力与其预测性能在此设定中呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重复尝试求解过程中的改进、平台期或退步的动力学对认知科学和自主智能体研究都至关重要。编程课程中的自动评分提供了天然的迭代反馈环境，但目前缺乏大规模、细粒度的数据来研究这一序列建模任务。

Method: 构建CodeInsight大规模数据集（超过300万次提交，含测试用例级结果、时间戳和源代码），并建立一个统一的校准与评分协议基准，涵盖参数化模型、序列模型（包括适配的RSSM，通过离散潜变量追踪求解者动态状态）以及LLM生成式预测器。

Result: 适配的RSSM在四个课程中的三个上达到最高的预测准确率。LLM预测器准确率较低但能生成完整提交，支持直接的失败模式分析。研究发现LLM的编码熟练度与预测性能呈负相关，表明LLM在此场景中更适合作为条件生成求解器而非求解者行为的忠实预测器。

Conclusion: CodeInsight为迭代问题求解的序列建模研究提供了大规模基准资源；RSSM类状态空间模型在预测求解行为方面优于生成式LLM，后者更适合作为生成求解器使用。代码和数据在请求后公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Solving problems through repeated attempts is a sequential modeling task: at each step, the solver receives feedback and decides how to revise their solutions. Predicting whether performance improves, plateaus, or regresses across attempts is central to understanding any iterative problem-solving process in both human learners and autonomous agents. Beyond outcomes, modeling what errors persist and how strategies shift across attempts provides deeper insight into the mechanics of sequential learning. Studying these dynamics requires observing many solvers as they attempt, receive feedback, and revise. Programming courses with automated grading provide this setting, as students iteratively submit code to test suites and receive feedback on every attempt. We therefore curate CodeInsight, a large-scale dataset of over 3 million submissions from 3,286 undergraduates across 2 introductory C++ courses in 2 academic years, with test-case-level outcomes, timestamps, and source code. On this dataset, we build a benchmark that evaluates models spanning parametric, sequential, and generative traditions under a shared calibration-and-scoring protocol, including a Recurrent State Space Model (RSSM) adapted to track solver characteristics through discrete latent variables and an LLM-based predictor that generates explicit solutions. The adapted RSSM achieves the strongest predictive accuracy on three of the four courses. The LLM predictor is less accurate but produces full submissions at each attempt, enabling direct analysis of failure modes. We find that the model's coding proficiency is inversely related to predictive performance in this setting, with the LLM better understood as a generative solver conditioned on context rather than a faithful predictor of solver behavior. We publicly release our code and the dataset on request to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [158] [Calibration is the Bottleneck: An Action-Class Diagnostic of Multi-Turn Tool-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00949)
*Kangjia Zhao,Jiajun Li,Haozhan Shen,Wei Chow,Linfeng Li,Hang Song,Lingdong Kong,Chen Zhi,Tiancheng Zhao,Songhua Liu,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向动作类（action-class）的诊断框架，将多轮工具调用的失败分解为"动作类误校准"和"动作执行失败"两种正交模式，并引入自揭示上界 Acc ≤ GAR（金标动作召回率）来区分二者。在多个多轮基准和一组模型中验证后发现，动作类误校准是被状态评分器掩盖的重大失败模式，而仅通过上下文扰动即可重塑校准，但重塑效果具有异质性（同一扰动在不同模型族上可使准确率变化从+11.5到-21.0个百分点不等），因此多轮工具调用评测应补充动作类诊断而非只看聚合准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开工具调用基准上，开源模型在聚合准确率上已逼近甚至超越闭源前沿模型，但该指标将多种多轮情境取平均，掩盖了各情境下进展是否均衡的问题，无法揭示模型失败的具体机理。

Method: 提出动作类导向的诊断框架：在四类动作空间（TOOL_CALL/ASK/REFUSE/CONFIRM）上操作，将多轮失败分解为动作类误校准与动作执行失败两种正交模式；引入自揭示上界 Acc ≤ GAR（Gold Action Recall），通过上界违反（Acc > GAR，暴露状态评分器掩盖误校准）和上界松弛（GAR >> Acc，将执行失败定位在TOOL_CALL内）识别两类模式；在多个多轮基准上对一组工具调用模型进行验证。

Result: 诊断揭示动作类误校准是状态评分器无法看到的重大失败模式，该差距抬高了重度工具训练模型家族的排名；仅通过上下文扰动即可重塑校准，但重塑效果具异质性——同一扰动在相同场景下对不同模型家族产生的准确率变化方向相反（+11.5 vs -21.0 pp），且效果还取决于扰动机制。

Conclusion: 多轮工具调用评测应超越聚合准确率，补充能够暴露模型在每个情境中实际行为的动作类诊断指标。

Abstract: Multi-turn tool calling is a core evaluation scenario for large language model (LLM) agents. On public tool-calling benchmarks, open-weight models now approach or even surpass closed-source frontier models in aggregate accuracy. However, this metric averages over many different multi-turn situations and obscures whether progress is balanced across them. We propose an action-class-oriented diagnostic framework that decomposes multi-turn failures into two orthogonal modes: action-class miscalibration and action-execution failure. The framework operates over a four-class action space (TOOL_CALL/ASK/REFUSE/CONFIRM) and introduces a self-revealing upper bound Acc <= GAR (Gold Action Recall); the two modes show up as bound violation (Acc > GAR, exposing state-grader masking of miscalibration) and large bound slack (GAR >> Acc, localizing execution failure within TOOL_CALL). We validate it on a panel of tool-calling models across multiple multi-turn benchmarks. Across our panel, the diagnostic reveals action-class miscalibration as a substantial failure mode the state grader cannot see. This gap inflates standing for heavily tool-trained families, which our diagnostic separates from families with context-appropriate action choice. Calibration is reshapable through context-only perturbations, but the reshape is heterogeneous: a single perturbation moves accuracy in opposite directions across families (up to +11.5 vs -21.0 pp on the same scenario), and its effect further depends on the perturbation mechanism. We argue that multi-turn tool-calling evaluations should supplement aggregate accuracy with action-class diagnostics that expose what the model actually does in each scenario.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TPGC，一种双先验（任务先验+结构先验）驱动的prompt初始化方法，用于解决多任务图预训练框架中随机初始化prompt与预训练目标及图结构对齐不良的问题。TPGC通过任务先验注入模块进行同构多任务预训练继承优化偏好，再通过结构先验注入模块提取迁移性全局结构上下文并转化为层间prompt向量。在6个基准上，TPGC在少样本设置下以更少可调参数和更低运行时间取得优于SOTA的一致表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图prompt学习在多任务图预训练框架中通常使用随机初始化的prompt，导致prompt空间与预训练任务目标及图结构特征之间存在较差的语义对齐，严重削弱了prompt表征的任务相关性、结构感知性与可迁移性。

Method: 提出TPGC双先验prompt初始化框架：(1) Task-Prior Injection Module在辅助图上进行短暂的同类多任务预训练，使prompt初始化继承多任务优化偏好；(2) Structure-Prior Injection Module基于任务感知表征提取可迁移的全局结构上下文，通过聚合结构信息丰富的节点嵌入生成层间prompt向量，显式建模任务先验与结构先验的协同作用。

Result: 在覆盖节点分类与图分类的6个主流基准数据集上，TPGC在少样本（few-shot）设置下相比SOTA基线取得持续更优性能，同时所需下游可调参数更少、运行时间更低。

Conclusion: TPGC通过显式注入任务先验与结构先验来初始化prompt，有效解决了随机prompt与图预训练目标及结构特征的对齐问题，为图prompt学习提供了高效且可迁移的新范式。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [160] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REAL-Q是一种新型后训练量化（PTQ）范式，通过端到端对齐的全局损失代理函数配合细粒度动态块级梯度下降（每128列更新一次），并引入滑动窗口机制实现平滑的跨层过渡，从而在W4A16设置下显著降低LLM的量化误差，将端到端KL散度相对先进全局引导方法降低约49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的PTQ方法在量化每层时使用单一的闭式二阶求解器，为保持解析可解性不得不严重近似全局损失（丢弃跨通道耦合、合并输出行成组），且冻结Hessian在整个层内不变，无法随损失景观的逐列变化而刷新——作者将此称为"信息错位"问题。

Method: REAL-Q不再为了解析可解性而稀释目标函数，而是直接面向端到端对齐的全局损失代理函数，通过细粒度的动态块级梯度下降（每处理128列即应用一次修正）来优化；同时结合滑动窗口机制实现平滑的跨层过渡，抑制误差在网络中的传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B和70B）以及Qwen3（0.6B-32B）系列模型上，采用W4A16量化设置，REAL-Q相比最先进的全局引导方法将端到端KL散度相对降低约49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q通过打破"以牺牲精度换取解析可解性"的传统妥协，证明了动态细粒度梯度修正与滑动窗口跨层衔接的有效性，为LLM PTQ提供了新的研究范式，在资源受限场景下实现更强的量化性能。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [161] [Convergence issues in Relational Concept Analysis based on AOC-posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00054)
*Xavier Dolques,Agnès Braud,Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Florence Le Ber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在AOC-poset结构上实例化关系概念分析（RCA）时收敛性的丧失问题，揭示了收敛失败的原因、可确保收敛的条件、恢复收敛的数据变换方法，并提出一种保留AOC-poset结构的收敛变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCA原本定义在概念格上并保证迭代收敛，但当与更小更高效的AOC-poset结合时，收敛性不再有保证，这阻碍了RCA在需要AOC-poset（以缓解组合爆炸或聚焦于最具信息量的结构）的应用场景中的使用。因此需要深入理解并解决这一收敛性丧失问题。

Method: 作者系统地分析了AOC-poset上RCA收敛性丧失的原因，识别能够保证收敛的条件，讨论通过数据变换恢复收敛的可能性，并提出一种收敛变体：一旦创建关系属性便永不移除，从而以引入可能指向最终结构中不存在概念的多余属性为代价，保证收敛并保留AOC-poset结构。

Result: 证明了在一般情况下AOC-poset上RCA的收敛不再是必然的，给出了可确保收敛的特定条件，展示了数据集变换以恢复收敛的方法，并提出了一种保证收敛的变体算法，该变体通过保留所有已创建的关系属性来牺牲属性简洁性换取收敛保证。

Conclusion: 在概念格设置下RCA的收敛保证在AOC-poset中失效，但可以通过特定条件识别、数据变换或采用不删除关系属性的收敛变体来恢复或保证收敛，为在更高效的结构上应用RCA提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) is an approach for conceptual classification building and rule discovery from a binary table describing a set of objects by a set of attributes. Extensions have been proposed to deal with non-binary and more complex data, such as Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) for multi-relational data. RCA aims to highlight groups of objects characterized by their relationships with other groups of objects. The richer and more complex nature of the underlying data allows RCA to produce richer results than FCA, at the expense of higher computational and interpretive complexity. The most commonly used conceptual classification structure in FCA is the concept lattice. However, in many applications, concept lattice substructures, such as AOC-posets, are preferred over the full lattice, either to mitigate combinatorial blow-up or to focus on the most informative parts of the structure. Indeed, in an AOC-poset, only concepts introducing an object or an attribute are represented, which makes AOC-posets smaller and easier to compute and use than concept lattices. Although RCA was originally defined on concept lattices, it can also be instantiated on AOC-posets. RCA is iterative and its convergence is guaranteed in the lattice-based setting, but this guarantee is lost when using AOC-posets. In this paper, we investigate this loss of convergence in detail. We show why convergence is no longer guaranteed in the general case, identify conditions under which it can still be ensured, and discuss how a dataset can be transformed to recover convergence. We also propose a convergent variant of the process, which preserves the AOC-poset structure: relational attributes, once created, are never removed, which guarantees convergence at the price of attributes that may refer to concepts absent from the final structures.

</details>


### [162] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISTAL框架，一种用于结构无关（structure-agnostic）材料性质预测的双先验框架，结合自监督成分预训练与结构感知知识蒸馏，在39个基准任务上优于参考基准37个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料性质预测在低数据场景下仍具挑战性，许多目标性质仅有少量标注样本。现有精度最高的模型通常依赖晶体结构信息，这限制了其在早期筛选中（结构信息有限或缺失时）的应用。因此需要一种不依赖结构输入但仍能利用结构知识的预测方法。

Method: DISTAL采用双先验框架：(1) 自监督成分预训练——使用145个成分推导描述符，从大规模虚拟成分空间学习可迁移的成分表征；(2) 结构感知知识蒸馏——将预训练ALIGNN教师模型的结构知识蒸馏到成分条件化的学生模型中。训练时利用结构先验，推理时无需结构输入。最终将显式成分描述符、预训练潜特征和蒸馏结构特征在统一预测管线中多模态融合。

Result: 在39个基准任务中，最佳多模态配置（融合全部三种信号）在37个任务上优于参考基准，是所有特征组合中整体性能最强的。

Conclusion: 成分预训练与结构蒸馏提供互补先验，为小数据材料信息学中稳健的成分-only预测提供了实用路线。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [163] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReNFT方法，通过内部概率质量重新校准来修复扩散生成器中已发生模式坍缩的高奖励、低多样性适配器，无需任何外部信号干预。在保留99%左右奖励的同时，将DreamSim多样性格提升约55-59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励后训练会导致扩散生成器将概率质量集中到少数高奖励模式上，造成模式坍缩并损失提示内多样性。现有缓解方法（如感知目标增强、参考正则化调整、文本编码器修改等）都依赖外部信号或接口，且无法在保持高奖励的同时修复已坍缩的适配器。

Method: ReNFT基于"坍缩是抑制而非删除"的核心观察（在线后训练主要是在预训练继承的能力间重新分配概率质量）。方法包括：(1) 使用无条件探针优先选择"反枢纽"提示，使提示无关偏置最易暴露；(2) 从相同提示和初始噪声生成两条策略主导的混合路线——一条探测冻结基础方向以寻找被抑制的替代方案，另一条暴露后训练的无条件倾向；(3) 通过带自适应翻转守卫的奖励排序分配"拉/推"角色；(4) 执行联合配对的NFT更新完成修复。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval基准上，ReNFT分别保留了NFT 98.9%和99.0%的奖励，同时将DreamSim-Div多样性各提升58.8%和55.0%，证明其可作为外部干预方法的互补替代方案。

Conclusion: 扩散生成器中的模式坍缩本质上是概率质量在预训练已有能力间的重新分配（抑制而非删除），因此可以通过生成器内部机制实现可逆修复。ReNFT无需外部信号即可在几乎不损失奖励的前提下显著恢复多样性，为奖励后训练中的坍缩修复提供了新的内部校准路径。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward. We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [164] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了上下文学习（ICL）保持诊断中基于注意力代理指标的可靠性。作者提出两个新指标：ICS（上下文敏感度，度量最后token注意力对不同演示前缀的行距离）和ICL-GAP（行为准确率差距）。通过在Llama-2-7B上的四臂消融实验发现，最大化ICS的正则化器可使ICS接近其几何上限，但行为指标ICL-GAP几乎为零，MMLU准确率反而从0.371降至0.279，出现Goodhart解耦现象——即优化的注意力代理指标与真实行为目标发生背离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在微调后会削弱其上下文学习能力，现有保持诊断方法主要通过检查注意力变化来推断模型是否具有上下文敏感性。然而，一旦这些注意力代理被作为优化目标进行训练，其诊断可靠性尚不清楚。本文旨在检验在注意力代理被优化的情况下，基于注意力的ICL代理指标是否仍然可信。

Method: 作者形式化定义了两个指标：In-Context Sensitivity（ICS），即匹配与不匹配演示前缀下最后token注意力之间的平均行距离；以及ICL-GAP，即相同前缀之间的行为准确率差距。在Llama-2-7B上设计受控四臂消融实验，使用最大化ICS的正则化器，并通过端点统计、随机标签协议和行为门控等分析手段定位机制。

Result: ICS最大化正则化器将ICS驱动至1.413，达到其几何上限的99.5%。但行为指标显示ICL-GAP保持接近零，MMLU准确率从0.371降至0.279，表明出现Goodhart解耦。机制定位发现注意力变得尖锐且跨前缀近似不相交，但路由到格式化和演示体token而非标签token。行为门控可部分缓解该效应，而锚定预训练计算的目标能保持高MMLU、中等ICS区域。

Conclusion: 主要教训是诊断性的：基于注意力的ICL代理指标只有在针对行为差距验证后，才能作为训练目标使用。在缺乏行为验证的情况下优化注意力和解指标会导致Goodhart式背离，产生表面满足代理条件但实际损害模型行为的失败模式。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [165] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于随机游走的去中心化LoRA微调方法，通过单个模型令牌在网络中顺序更新，替代了集中式聚合和gossip式同步，显著降低通信与计算开销并避免聚合误差，同时提供非凸优化的收敛保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LoRA微调方法（集中式聚合和gossip式去中心化）都需要多次模型副本之间的频繁同步，产生显著的通信开销，并且同时聚合多个模型更新会引入聚合误差。

Method: 提出随机游走式LoRA微调方案：不维护多个模型副本，而是让单个模型令牌在网络中游走，在每个节点上使用本地微调目标进行顺序更新，从而消除全局同步需求。

Result: 在多个NLP任务和图拓扑上的实验表明，所提方法在取得竞争性任务性能的同时，通信和计算开销显著低于gossip式LoRA方法。

Conclusion: 随机游走式LoRA微调是一种高效的去中心化分布式微调方案，在避免同步和聚合开销的同时可以实现与现有方法相当的性能，并在标准假设下具有非凸目标的严格收敛保证。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [166] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于归一化最大似然（NML）模型计算包含聚类结构的编码向量的随机概率（最短编码长度）问题，提出了一种递归公式来高效计算NML模型的归一化常数，将时间复杂度从多项式降低到线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于最小描述长度（MDL）原理的数据聚类中，计算包含聚类结构的向量的最短编码长度具有重要的理论与实践意义，可用于估计最佳聚类数目和最佳聚类结构。然而，基于NML模型的直接计算方法需要多项式时间，因此需要更高效的算法。

Method: 本文通过引入一个递归公式来高效计算NML模型中的归一化常数，从而实现了对最短编码长度的线性时间复杂度计算。该递归公式避免了直接的穷举式多项式计算方法。

Result: 提出的新公式将时间复杂度从先前关于向量大小和聚类数目的多项式时间降为线性时间，证明该问题是可处理的（tractable）。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了基于NML模型计算聚类结构编码向量的随机概率是可高效求解的问题，所提出的线性时间递归公式为大规模数据聚类中的MDL准则应用提供了实用基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [167] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了五个基础模型家族的九个变体（零样本模式）与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型，在德国、波兰和西班牙市场2021-2025年的表现。结果表明仅TabPFN模型在所有统计指标上一致且显著优于基准，但统计优势并不直接转化为经济优势——TabPFN在高风险策略下最优，而分布式深度神经网络基准在低风险偏好下更有利可图。因此基础模型不能普遍替代市场专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型宣称能在几乎没有任务特定训练的情况下提供准确预测，但它们在电价预测领域能否取代专用模型这一问题尚不明确，本文旨在澄清这一点。

Method: 选取五个基础模型家族的九个变体以零样本模式进行评估，与两种最先进的电价预测基准模型在德国、波兰、西班牙三个市场的2021-2025年数据上进行对比。评估涵盖点预测和概率预测精度，以及电池储能套利中的经济价值。

Result: 仅TabPFN模型在所有三个市场、所有统计指标上一致且显著优于基准模型；但TabPFN仅在无限出价和较高风险的分位数策略下表现最佳，当风险容忍度较低时，分布式深度神经网络基准模型更有利可图。

Conclusion: 基础模型无法普遍取代市场专用预测模型；其价值取决于模型架构和具体的决策问题，统计性能优势不能直接等同于经济价值优势。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [168] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将特征重要性方法(FIMs)嵌入基于证据权重(Weight of Evidence, WoE)的假设检验框架中，量化观测证据对特征重要性假设的支持程度，从而实现对FIMs的原则性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FIMs（如LIME、SHAP）广泛用于可解释AI，但仅依赖归因分数难以深入理解模型推理过程，缺乏对FIMs的严格评估机制。

Method: 将FIMs嵌入基于WoE的假设检验框架，参考假设可来源于领域知识、真实标签或FIM自身；通过该框架同时捕获FIM与先验知识的一致性及其变异性，并提供WoE与归因方差之间的理论联系。

Result: 在LIME和SHAP上、不同参考假设设定下验证了所提框架的适用性和灵活性；理论结果建立了WoE与归因方差的关联。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估FIMs提供了一种基于对比证据的补充性工具，突破了传统归因分数局限。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [169] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safin-1基础模型家族，通过记忆锚定路由(MARCH)架构和状态演化实现"内在安全"(Safety from Within)，将安全视为模型自身的原生计算能力而非外部约束，并通过可演化的能力状态支持测试时自适应安全调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程复杂任务要求基础模型在长时间交互中持续积累信息、维护内部状态并自适应。传统做法将安全视为外部防护栏或事后对齐(如监督微调)的外在行为约束，而非模型内在属性。这导致安全能力难以随交互动态演化。作者认为安全应当是模型自身的固有属性，需要通过与模型原生计算相融合的安全相关能力表示与调用来实现。

Method: 提出Safin-1模型族，其核心机制包括：(1) MARCH(Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History)网络架构，维持结构化记忆状态并通过内容条件路由选择性检索相关历史信息；(2) 支持测试时自适应，通过持久化能力状态的演化而不重复修改骨干网络，实现受控特化；(3) 引入Safety State作为下游安全任务的安全状态接口，在模型原生计算内统一上下文记忆与持久能力自适应，将记忆从被动历史记录转变为主动行为维护与演化的基质。

Result: 在安全任务上通过Safety State进行状态自适应表现出实质性安全改进；在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索和效率等方面的评估验证了Safin-1的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将安全作为状态原生且可自适应维护能力的初步路径，但作者强调这仅是"内在安全"的初始架构探索，实现更广泛愿景仍需大量后续研究。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [170] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Local Reference Geometry (LRG)，一种轻量级post-hoc特征增强模块，用于诊断和修复不平衡时间序列分类中少数类样本局部特征空间几何失效的问题。LRG基于训练特征，通过测量局部暴露度和类别混合风险，为标准化的符号位移和LDA投影残差添加增强信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不平衡时间序列分类方法大多通过改变训练分布、目标函数、logits或最终阈值来应对偏差，但忽略了表征层面的问题：在少数类支持减少后，学习到的特征空间在少数类区域是否保持局部可靠性。作者识别出训练局部的几何失效：不平衡条件下，即使表征保留全局类别结构，少数类样本仍可能处于稀疏、被多数类主导或混合的特征空间邻域中。

Method: LRG作为固定特征提取器与分类头之间的轻量级post-hoc特征增强模块。它仅使用训练特征，测量局部暴露度和类别混合风险，然后将每个固定特征与来自附近训练几何的标准符号位移及LDA投影残差摘要进行增强，之后再送入分类器。

Result: 在UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上，成对的raw-versus-LRG对比显示对学习型、预训练型和固定特征表示均有增益，且可与训练层面的干预和后编码器分类器修正结合使用。消融实验表明增益来自附加到原始特征上的符号局部残差，而非通用的原型距离、亲和特征、标量统计量或VLAD风格编码。分析支持局部几何失效假说：不平衡导致少数类邻域越来越多地被多数类暴露，训练局部风险能识别错误易发区域，LRG增益集中在这些高风险区域。

Conclusion: LRG作为一个有效且可解释的post-hoc特征增强方案，填补了不平衡时间序列分类中表征层面局部几何可靠性诊断与修复的空白。它证明不平衡分类的性能提升不仅来自训练分布或决策边界的干预，还可来自对表征空间中少数类局部几何结构的显式修复，且该修复在不同类型的特征表示上均表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [171] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FFD是一个硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过全融合内核、低比特量化的内容感知扫描和top-delta动态稀疏策略，突破长上下文解码中的内存带宽瓶颈，实现最高11.6倍内核级加速和2.37倍端到端吞吐提升，可扩展到256K上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM的解码过程受限于内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制的二次复杂度。现有方法中，基于元数据的指标存在内存开销问题，而自适应选择策略则存在计算低效问题，二者之间存在固有的权衡。作者希望打破这种权衡，解决长上下文解码中的"内存墙"问题。

Method: FFD采用硬件-算法协同设计框架，核心创新包括：(1) 将选择器与计算器集成到全融合的内核中，用低比特量化的内容感知扫描替代外部元数据索引；(2) 提出top-delta策略，通过动态过滤块实现分布自适应稀疏，且无需全局同步；(3) 支持扫描结果复用进行计算，实现训练无关的即插即用方案。

Result: FFD在kernel级别实现了最高11.6倍加速，可扩展到256K上下文长度，端到端吞吐量提升2.37倍。在RULER和LongBench基准上的实验验证表明，FFD在保持模型精度的同时实现了高比例稀疏性。

Conclusion: FFD有效证明了硬件-算法协同设计在突破长上下文解码内存墙方面的可行性，提供了一种无需训练、即插即用的高效长上下文推理加速方案，在保持模型精度的前提下显著提升了解码速度和吞吐量，代码已开源。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [172] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向腐蚀领域知识综合的领域适配检索增强生成（RAG）框架，在镁合金腐蚀数据集上微调三种开源语言模型，并引入Reason Map命题图框架来检测因果方向倒置和无依据推理跳跃，从而实现不仅准确检索且具备机制可辩护推理的腐蚀预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，可靠预测对及时缓解至关重要。机器学习虽能有效预测腐蚀速率但无法解释底层机制；在安全关键的工程材料领域，需要的不只是准确检索，还包括机制上可辩护的推理。然而现有事实性指标无法评估这种能力，因此需要一种同时兼顾准确性检索和机制推理能力的框架。

Method: (1) 构建包含3,309个专家验证问答对的领域语料库（来自840篇同行评审论文）；(2) 微调三种开源语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B）；(3) 集成混合稠密-词法检索管道；(4) 提出Reason Map——一种命题图框架，从生成答案和检索文献中独立构建有向证据图，检测因果方向倒置和无依据推理跳跃。

Result: 检索增强带来Token F1提升143-194%；系统忠实度为0.964，上下文召回率为0.988；盲外部验证（新发表文献与自建电化学数据）确认了趋势级泛化能力。

Conclusion: Reason Map能系统性地检测平面事实性指标无法暴露的因果方向和推理缺陷；模块化架构可跨领域应用，为可信AI辅助知识综合提供了通用蓝图，不仅适用于腐蚀规避，也可推广至其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [173] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECCBbench基准与评估协议，从效率（FLOPs）、压缩（可压缩输入是否被更好记忆）和校准（系统是否根据不确定性和错误成本弃权）三个维度衡量超越系统容量之外的内存质量，发现预训练VLM在文本上能压缩记忆但在视频上不能，且两者校准都很差；多种非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM/VLM记忆基准仅用长文本或视频的准确率来衡量记忆能力，但准确率无法捕捉真实长时程任务中重要的属性。记忆应被理解为容量之外的系统性能力，需要一个新的评估框架来全面衡量。

Method: 引入ECCBench基准和评估协议，提出ECC三维评估框架：(1) 效率（Efficiency）——从记忆回答所需计算量（FLOPs）；(2) 压缩（Compression）——可压缩输入是否被更准确或更高效地记忆；(3) 校准（Calibration）——系统是否根据自身不确定性和错误成本选择弃权。该协议超越了传统的容量限制下准确率评估。

Result: 预训练VLM在文本上能够进行记忆压缩，但在视频上不能，并且在文本和视频上的校准都很差。在更广泛的记忆骨干网络对比中，多种非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformers。

Conclusion: ECC维度揭示的记忆属性与单独准确率不同，非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上的优势暗示它们可能成为长时程代理系统中有用的组件。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [174] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution"（缺陷于自然，完美于进化）的机制：通过对AI/ML模型系数施加刻意突变（偏离最优解），使群体模型在非平稳环境中作为统计对冲，可持续地提升整体性能。论文通过四个定理从理论上保证遗憾（regret）降低，并在合成线性回归任务上验证了该机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界问题常常发生不可预测的漂移，导致AI/ML模型在训练环境与部署环境不一致时性能退化。生物进化通过自然选择作用于遗传变异解决了这一问题，但AI/ML领域虽然早已引入自然选择思想，却因优化天然趋向收敛而难以维持模型多样性，缺乏在动态环境中可持续适应的机制。

Method: 论文提出通过对模型系数施以刻意的、偏离最优性的突变，构建一个模型群体（swarm），使其作为非平稳环境的统计对冲。作者通过四个定理证明在一般条件下遗憾降低是有保证的，从而将"缺陷于自然"确立为AI/ML系统可泛化的设计原则。同时设计了一个简单的自适应控制器，用于调节突变漂移率以匹配环境中未知的漂移率，并采用推理合成（inference synthesis）将群体的个体优势转化为集体优势。

Result: 在合成线性回归任务上，突变群体在约80%的环境变化中提供了最佳模型；推理合成成功将个体优势转化为集体性能提升。实验表明该机制在突变漂移率与环境漂移率匹配时最为有效。

Conclusion: "Flawed-in-Nature"机制与生物进化的紧密类比暗示它可能是有机发现更接近生物智能的人工智能形式所缺失的关键成分，可作为动态环境下AI/ML系统鲁棒性设计的一般性原则。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [175] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT)，一种无需轨迹级强化学习的统一分子生成优化方法。它通过奖励驱动的精英选择（选取得分高的分子）配合模型自身的预训练损失进行微调，可跨多种模型架构（自回归、掩码扩散、离散流）和多种设计任务（从头生成、基序延伸、连接子设计）直接复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导向的分子优化通常依赖基于策略梯度的强化学习，但该方法需要计算生成轨迹的对数概率，其形式取决于模型架构和生成过程，导致优化器难以跨不同架构和条件生成设计复用。监督微调虽无需此机制，但更新由固定数据集驱动，奖励信号无法进入更新过程。因此需要一种既能引入奖励信息、又不依赖特定架构轨迹计算的统一优化方法。

Method: EW-SFT通过奖励引导的精英选择策略，从生成的分子中筛选出高分样本构成精英集，然后仅在该精英集上以模型自身的预训练损失（无需额外强化学习机制）进行监督微调。通过消融实验验证奖励信息主要通过精英二元选择传递，而非在精英集内的连续加权。由于更新过程只消耗已评分的分子和模型原生损失，该方法可应用于自回归、掩码扩散和离散流生成器，以及从头设计、基序延伸和连接子设计等多种任务。

Result: 在固定预算的3D形状比对oracle调用（基于两种激酶参考化合物）下，EW-SFT一致优于各架构对应的原生优化器。在4个留出参考化合物的2D相似性oracle上进一步提升了目标导向优化性能。在样本效率基准测试中取得与轨迹级RL公式相当的表现。

Conclusion: EW-SFT是一个统一且有效的分子生成优化器，可跨不同分子生成器架构、设计约束、参考化合物和oracle类型发挥作用，无需轨迹级强化学习公式化。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [176] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weight-Harness Alternating Learning (WHALE)，一种交替优化模型权重与智能体可执行harness代码（上下文管理与控制流）的联合适应方法。通过在线拒绝采样微调（更新模型）与Meta-Harness（搜索harness）交替进行，在三个领域（搜索问答、数学推理、国际象棋谜题）上均优于仅权重、仅harness及Fast-Slow Training基线，最佳mean@8准确率提升4.15-24.38个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的性能同时取决于模型参数和执行harness代码（管理上下文与控制流）。现有方法仅联合优化权重和文本提示，但将更广泛的harness视为固定不变，导致任一组件成为系统瓶颈。权重更新可能改变有效的harness，harness更新也可能改变暴露的模型能力，因此需要真正的权重-harness联合适应。

Method: WHALE交替执行两个阶段：(1) 在当前harness下通过在线拒绝采样微调更新模型；(2) 在新模型下搜索更优的harness，使用Meta-Harness实现。关键设计是何时切换阶段——采用固定阶段时长或基于训练信号的自适应patience规则，以区分真实改进与噪声，同时避免对变化中的对手过度优化。还对比了交错小步更新与阶段式先权重后harness的优化策略。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体，在搜索问答、数学推理和国际象棋谜题三个领域上，WHALE的最佳mean@8准确率超过仅权重、仅harness及Fast-Slow Training基线4.15-24.38个百分点。具体发现：在SearchQA中harness搜索用更少的rollout就达到仅权重优化的峰值准确率；数学准确率仅在权重更新后才获提升；小步交错更新在准确率和rollout成本上均优于阶段式优化。

Conclusion: 模型权重与harness代码都可能成为系统瓶颈，交替联合优化两者是一种简单有效的策略。WHALE证明了组件间的协同适应能带来显著的性能增益，且切换策略（固定时长或自适应patience）是分离真实改进与噪声的关键设计决策。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [177] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QTEA是一个面向LLM的sub-2-bit权重后训练量化(PTQ)框架，将权重量化为三值并用显著权重作为残差补偿器（采用1:4半结构化稀疏），结合列级缩放细化和错误衰减机制，在Qwen3-14B和Llama3-8B上超越了现有三元PTQ基线，并通过查表内核实现7.2倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的权重后训练量化方法在低于2比特时往往遭受严重精度损失，且难以在不同模型间泛化。虽然许多方法利用非结构化稀疏性缓解精度下降，但这牺牲了执行规律性和GPU友好性。作者希望在不牺牲硬件效率的前提下实现sub-2-bit的量化精度。

Method: QTEA包含四个关键设计：(1) 将权重量化为三元值（ternary values），使用显著权重作为残差误差补偿器，残差被分配到选择列并施加1:4半结构化稀疏以保证硬件效率；(2) 将列级缩放细化整合到GPTQ风格的逐列量化中，交替更新每列缩放因子和三元分配以最小化重构误差；(3) 识别GPTQ中与顺序相关的误差传播问题，引入错误衰减机制减弱后期误差累积；(4) 开发基于查找表的内核加速推理。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上，QTEA将所有权重压缩至有效1.7比特/权重，相比最强三元PTQ基线平均准确率提升16.7%，WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低1.40×和2.61×。在Llama3-8B上获得6.6%准确率增益，困惑度分别降低1.34×和1.95×。基于查表的核实现相比FP16基线实现7.2×逐token生成加速。

Conclusion: QTEA通过在sub-2-bit条件下结合三元量化、1:4半结构化稀疏残差补偿、列级缩放细化和错误衰减，在保持GPU友好硬件效率的同时显著提升了极低比特量化的精度，证明了极低比特PTQ在实际LLM部署中的可行性和实用性。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [178] [Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive Generative Model for PDEs in Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00297)
*Zi Wang,Minghui Xu,Tapan Mukerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoLAMP，一种几何感知的潜在自回归生成模型，用于解决微米级高度复杂曲折结构中的多物理场PDE问题。该模型采用图表示上的双编码器架构联合捕获全局拓扑和细尺度几何特征，并在潜空间中利用带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer进行时序建模，实现稳定可扩展的分块自回归预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解多物理场偏微分方程在科学计算中仍是一大挑战，尤其是涉及能量与化学工程中高度复杂的微米级曲折几何结构时，传统数值方法难以高效处理此类极度不规则几何中的物理仿真。

Method: GeoLAMP包含三个核心组件：(1) 在图上构建双编码器架构，联合捕获全局拓扑与细尺度几何特征，实现从实空间场到紧凑潜表示的转换；(2) 在潜空间中使用带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer建模时序动态，支持稳定且可扩展的分块自回归预测；(3) 灵活的解码器在任意点上重建高分辨率物理场。

Result: 作者建立了三个复杂几何中的多物理场基准数据集（反应流、热对流和弹性力学），GeoLAMP在所有数据集上均展示出最稳定的自回归性能，在整个滚动预报时域内保持低误差。

Conclusion: 本工作为微米级复杂几何中PDE的几何感知学习提供了系统性研究，并通过流匹配框架对潜自回归PDE建模的分块时间推进提出了新见解。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering. We address this challenge by proposing a Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive generative Model for PDEs (GeoLAMP) for solving physics within highly irregular and tortuous structures. GeoLAMP introduces a dual-encoder architecture on graph representations to jointly capture global topology and fine-scale geometric features, enabling an effective transition from real-space fields to compact latent representations. In the latent space, we propose a causal self-attention transformer with flow matching to model temporal dynamics, allowing stable and scalable block-wise autoregressive prediction. A flexible decoder reconstructs high-resolution physical fields on arbitrary points. We establish three multiphysics benchmark datasets in complex geometries, covering reactive flow, heat convection, and elasticity. GeoLAMP consistently achieves the most stable autoregression performance on these datasets, maintaining low errors throughout the entire rollout horizon. Our results provide a systematic study of geometry-aware learning for PDEs in $μ$m-scale complex geometries and offer new insights into block-wise time marching of latent autoregressive PDE modeling via a flow matching framework.

</details>


### [179] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了零样本大语言模型（LLM）在预测美国四个都市区的人口普查街区组级聚合移动性方面的能力，发现LLM预测精度（最佳0.435）显著低于监督学习基线（平均0.580）。通过方向一致性分析发现，LLM常依赖粗糙、稳定的先验知识（包括对受保护群体预测变量的不对称处理），虽能部分从城市背景中恢复聚合移动模式，但其预测不应被视为结构性可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度的人类移动轨迹数据通常属于专有、受限且隐私敏感的数据，难以广泛获取。LLM可能通过生成合理移动轨迹和预测个体移动来作为替代方案，但其能否推断聚合的邻域级移动模式尚不清楚。研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用四个美国都市区的匿名化Cuebiq数据，构建三种尺度的移动性结果变量（点位级、轨迹级和时间级），结合社会人口学与建筑环境预测因子。采用零样本LLM与监督基线（如OLS）进行对比，并引入方向性对齐分析（directional alignment analysis）检验LLM隐含的预测因子效应是否与经验OLS及Jonckheere-Terpstra趋势一致。

Result: 监督模型平均准确率为0.580，优于最佳LLM的0.435。空间范围结果的可预测性最强，但LLM与基线之间的差距也最大。方向性分析显示LLM常依赖跨结果和跨城市相似、粗糙且稳定的先验，且对受保护群体预测变量存在不对称处理。

Conclusion: LLM能够部分从城市背景中恢复聚合移动模式，但其预测不应被视为结构性可靠（structurally grounded）。在实际应用前需审计LLM预测的经验对齐性和潜在偏差，避免将LLM输出作为无需验证的地面真值使用。

Abstract: Human mobility is central to urban planning, transportation, public health, and emergency response, yet fine-grained trajectory data are often proprietary, restricted, and privacy-sensitive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by generating plausible mobility traces and predicting individual movement, but their ability to infer aggregate neighborhood-level mobility remains unclear. We evaluate zero-shot LLMs on Census Block Group-level mobility prediction across four U.S. metropolitan areas using anonymized Cuebiq data to construct point-level, trajectory-level, and temporal mobility outcomes, paired with sociodemographic and built-environment predictors. We compare LLM predictions with supervised baselines and introduce a directional alignment analysis to test whether LLM-implied predictor effects agree with empirical OLS and Jonckheere-Terpstra trends. Supervised models achieve 0.580 average accuracy, compared with 0.435 for the best LLM, with spatial extent outcomes showing the strongest predictability but also the largest LLM-baseline gaps. Directional analysis shows that LLMs often rely on coarse, stable predictor-level priors that remain similar across outcomes and cities, including asymmetric treatment of protected-group predictors. Overall, LLMs can partially recover aggregate mobility patterns from urban context, but their predictions should not be treated as structurally grounded without auditing empirical alignment and potential bias.

</details>


### [180] [Deterministic LLM Inference Across GPU Kernels: Power-of-Two INT8 Quantization Scales and the Limits of Tolerance-Based Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00363)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过向Qwen3-1.7B的INT8 GEMM流水线注入九种故障，系统评估了基于容差的量化内核一致性测试套件的能力边界。研究发现五类epilogue故障（缩放精度、双重舍入、乘法顺序、输出截断、融合顺序）的输出偏差最多不超过一个bfloat16间隔，导致容差为一个间隔的测试对该类故障基本失明；而违反累加器精确性前提或破坏操作数共享的故障则被无一例外地检测到。文章还发现将权重缩放因子强制为2的幂可使CUTLASS与Triton在所有线性层位级一致（196/196和252/252），并产生字节级相同的token序列，且之前报告的+157%困惑度恶化是由于探针未对权重重新量化所致，实际归因于幂次约束本身的比例仅0.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化GEMM内核的一致性测试套件通常约定两个实现在容差范围内一致即视为通过，但这类容差测试究竟能检测到什么、检测不到什么，尚缺乏系统性的实证分析。作者旨在刻画此类套件的能力边界，厘清"容差内一致"所真正确立的语义内涵（而非简单的可互换性），并探索可部署的干预手段以增强检测能力。
