Result: Sink Strength在六组VLM-LLM配对及多个格式敏感任务上一致地跟踪相对退化程度。后训练QK-RMSNorm注入无法复现原生QK-RMSNorm的保护效果，多种现成权重合并设置也无法在VL训练后恢复丢失的能力。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了在VL训练前使用Sink Strength筛选骨干模型的价值，并将干预空间收窄至训练过程中针对注意力头（head-selective）的保护策略。这些负面结果说明事后诊断和修复（post-hoc）手段效果有限，训练前的评估与训练中的保护更为关键。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a pretrained LLM into a vision-language model (VLM) can erode the backbone's text capability, with the damage concentrated on tasks that require following exact output rules, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning graded on a strictly parsed final answer, and similar evaluations with strict graders. We trace this gap to attention-sink corruption: VL fine-tuning perturbs the early sink position that anchors a large fraction of attention probability, and how well the base LLM preserves its sink tracks how much of the affected capability survives adaptation. Building on this view, we introduce Sink Strength, a single scalar computed on the base LLM in a few seconds on a single GPU that predicts post-VL degradation without any VL training. It consistently tracks relative degradation across the six VLM-LLM pairs and multiple format-sensitive tasks. Complementing this diagnostic, we find that post-pretraining QK-RMSNorm injection fails to reproduce the protection of native QK-RMSNorm, while several off-the-shelf weight-merging settings fail to recover the lost capability after VL training. These negative results underscore the value of screening backbones with Sink Strength before VL training and narrow the intervention space toward head-selective training-time protection.

</details>


### [215] [How Do Language Models Choose Between Context and Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00753)
*Benjamin Shih,John Winnicki,Arianna Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过反事实实验，探究大语言模型中"权威方向"(authority directions)是否能因果性地决定模型遵循上下文还是遵循参数化知识。研究表明，在同一任务内干预这些方向能复现30-68%的权威诱导行为偏移，但跨任务迁移仅能关闭9%的差异（而局部方向可关闭57%），表明权威计算可能是任务依赖的，而非跨任务可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明可通过激活方向(activation directions)来解码和引导模型在上下文信息与参数知识冲突时选择跟随哪一来源，但这类干预缺乏因果性验证——不确定未编辑模型是否天然利用该方向，也不确定该方向能否跨任务复用。

Method: 在无歧义设定下进行反事实实验：(1) 从一致提示(agreement prompts，即上下文和参数知识指向相同答案)中估计权威方向；(2) 在配对提示之间，对这些方向上的自然坐标进行坐标互换(interchange)，以操控模型优先跟随上下文还是参数知识；(3) 在Qwen、Llama和OLMo三个模型族上验证因果性；(4) 在两项独立任务上分别学习权威方向，测试跨任务迁移性。

Result: 方向干预可复现30-68%的权威诱导的来源选择偏移，匹配对照几乎无复现效果；跨任务迁移仅关闭9%的权威差距，而当前任务上学习的局部方向可关闭57%。

Conclusion: 权威方向在单一任务内具有因果效力，但其计算可能是任务依赖的，缺乏跨任务的因果可复用性——这一发现区分了权威的表征(representation)、因果使用(causal use)与跨任务因果复用(cross-task causal reuse)三个层面。

Abstract: When contextual information conflicts with the knowledge stored in model parameters, activation directions can be used to decode and steer which source the model follows. However, steering along a direction does not establish causality: whether the unedited model would naturally use that direction or whether the direction is reusable across tasks. We test these distinctions through counterfactual experiments in unambiguous settings. First, we estimate authority directions from agreement prompts, in which the context and parametric knowledge support the same answer. We then interchange naturally occurring coordinates along these directions between matched prompts that direct the model to prioritize either the supplied context or its parametric knowledge. Across Qwen, Llama, and OLMo models, this intervention reproduces 30-68% of the authority-induced shift in source choice, whereas matched controls reproduce almost none. To test cross-task reuse, we learn authority directions on two tasks separately and see that cross-task transferability closes only 9% of the authority gap while the local direction learned on the given task closes 57%. These results distinguish authority representation, causal use, and cross-task causal reuse, and suggest that authority computations may be task-dependent, rather than reusable across tasks.

</details>


### [216] [Frozen Cores Need Task Signal: Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance for Low-Resource LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00762)
*Wentao Ye,Zhanming Shen,Zhiqing Xiao,Yao Ding,Haobo Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FCCA方法，通过冻结核心（frozen-core）适配框架，在校准阶段固定左右基矩阵后仅微调r×r核心参数。该方法利用带符号的输入-误差交叉协方差并结合对角Fisher矩白化，在局部度量中选择子空间方向。实验表明在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分数，比次优方法高2.3分，且相比LoRA/DoRA仅用0.5%的训练参数即可达到接近的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（PEFT）通常只关注参数数量的预算问题，但忽视了在严格的训练状态预算下，参数位置（即哪些系数被更新）同样重要。现有方法中可训练因子能够修复初始子空间选择不良的问题，这掩盖了子空间质量本身的影响。作者希望直接观测和衡量固定子空间的质量，探索在极低参数预算下如何选择最优的固定子空间。

Method: 提出FCCA（Frozen-Core Cross-Covariance Adaptation）方法。具体流程：(1) 校准阶段为每个权重矩阵固定左右基（left/right basis）；(2) 估计带符号的输入-误差交叉协方差；(3) 使用对角Fisher矩对该协方差进行白化；(4) 在得到的局部度量中截断选择方向；(5) 将选定方向映射回原空间；(6) 应用薄QR分解获得稳定的核心坐标。最终仅微调r×r核心参数，而非传统地更新左右因子。

Result: 在8种基构造方法、11个任务、4种模型设置、3个种子的系统比较中：FCCA在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分，超过次优方法2.3分，并且在其未白化的RawGrad控制方法上11个任务全部超出。在三个Qwen规模上均排名第一，在Llama-3.2-1B上与前最佳方法差距仅0.13分。消融实验显示白化带来2.7-17.2分的提升，QR分解在测试环境下对稳定核心优化是必要的。FCCA与LoRA/DoRA平均差距仅0.32和0.23分，但仅优化36.9K参数而非约740万参数。

Conclusion: 精心选择的固定子空间能够恢复可移动低秩因子所带来收益的大部分，同时大幅降低可训练参数和优化器状态开销。子空间选择（而非仅仅参数数量）是低预算PEFT的关键因素，白化和正交化是其中不可或缺的组成部分。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is usually framed as a question of how many parameters to update. Under a severe trainable-state budget, however, where those coefficients act is equally consequential. We study this choice through frozen-core adaptation: a calibration pass fixes left and right bases for each weight matrix, and fine-tuning optimizes only an $r\times r$ core. This removes the ability of trainable factors to repair a poor initial span and makes subspace quality directly observable. We introduce FCCA, which estimates the signed input--error cross-covariance, whitens it with diagonal Fisher moments, truncates it in the resulting local metric, maps the selected directions back, and applies thin QR to obtain stable core coordinates. Under a matched $r^2$ budget, we compare eight basis constructors on 11 tasks, four model settings, and three seeds. On Qwen2.5-3B, FCCA reaches an 83.0 macro-average, 2.3 points above the next-best matched-budget constructor, and exceeds its unwhitened RawGrad control on all 11 tasks. It ranks first at all three Qwen scales and finishes within 0.13 points of the best method on Llama-3.2-1B. Controlled ablations show gains of 2.7--17.2 points from whitening and identify QR as necessary for stable core optimization in the tested regime. Finally, FCCA comes within 0.32 and 0.23 average points of LoRA and DoRA while optimizing 36.9K rather than roughly 7.4M parameters. These results show that a carefully selected fixed span can recover most of the benefit of movable low-rank factors at a much smaller trainable and optimizer-state cost.

</details>


### [217] [Are You Thinking What I am Thinking? : Examining Conceptual Separation in Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00764)
*Jaee Ponde,Roshni Agarwal,Subhashis Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究调查神经网络是否在其内部表示空间中展现"概念分离"——即同一概念的示例是否形成连贯表征，以及相关概念是否在表示空间中彼此更接近。通过分析CNN和LLM的内部激活，发现ImageNet概念在CNN中形成连贯且语义有序的表示，但该连贯性在未见概念和类别内域偏移下减弱；LLM中明显不同的域保持良好分离，相关子域更靠近，模糊主题的区分在均值与协方差层面均崩溃。研究表明概念分离能揭示仅凭输出精度无法发现的结构，可作为模型概念表示鲁棒性的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络评估主要依赖输出层面的指标（如分类准确率），但这些指标无法揭示概念在模型内部是如何被表征的。理解概念在表示空间中的组织方式对模型可解释性和鲁棒性至关重要，因此需要系统性地研究神经网络是否展现概念分离特性。

Method: 通过几何与分布层面的分析（包括均值和协方差结构），对CNN和LLM的内部激活进行考察。在CNN中使用ImageNet概念，在LLM中考察不同粒度的话题域（从清晰区分的域到模糊的主题），系统性评估概念表示的连贯性和语义有序性及其在不同条件（未见概念、域偏移）下的表现。

Result: CNN中，熟悉的ImageNet概念形成连贯且语义有序的表示，但这种连贯性在未见概念和类别内域偏移时减弱；LLM中，清晰不同的域保持良好分离，相关子域在表示空间中互相靠近，而模糊主题之间的区分在均值与协方差两个层面都发生崩溃。

Conclusion: 概念分离分析可以揭示仅靠输出准确率无法观察到的模型内部结构，并可作为评估模型表示概念鲁棒性的一种诊断工具。这种方法为理解神经网络的内部组织提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly employed to identify both well-defined and ambiguous concepts, yet output-level metrics reveal little about how those concepts are represented internally. Our study asks if these networks exhibit \textit{conceptual separation}: if examples of the same concept form coherent representations, and whether related concepts lie closer together in the representation space. We examine this conceptual organisation in Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) through geometric and distributional analysis of their internal activations. In CNNs, familiar ImageNet concepts form coherent and semantically ordered representations, while this coherence weakens for unseen concepts and suffers within-class domain shift. In LLMs, clearly distinct domains remain well separated, related subdomains move closer together, and the distinction between ambiguous topics collapses at both the mean and covariance level. These results suggest that conceptual separation can reveal structure that output accuracy alone cannot, and may serve as a useful diagnostic of how robustly a model represents the concepts it is asked to identify. Code and data available on \href{https://github.com/JaeeRoshniCapstoneProject/Are-You-Thinking-What-I-m-Thinking-Examining-Conceptual-Separation-in-Neural-Architectures}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [218] [Subspace Levenberg Marquardt Algorithms in Training Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00789)
*M. Duc Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了子空间Levenberg-Marquardt（LM）算法在神经网络回归和分类任务中的性能，并与经典LM方法及SGD、Adam等一阶算法进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LM算法在中小型神经网络上具有快速收敛和强鲁棒性，但随着网络参数增多，其计算和内存成本显著增加。虽然子空间方法（如KSLM和HSLM）已被提出以提高二阶算法的效率，但这些子空间LM变体在回归和分类任务中的系统评估仍然缺乏。

Method: 对子空间LM算法（包括Krylov子空间LM和混合子空间LM）在神经网络回归与分类任务中进行实验评估，并将这些子空间LM变体与经典LM方法以及主流一阶优化算法（SGD和Adam）进行性能对比。

Result: 论文提供了子空间LM变体与经典LM及一阶算法（SGD、Adam）在回归和分类任务上的对比实验结果，展示了子空间方法在保持二阶优化优势的同时提升效率的效果。

Conclusion: 子空间LM算法作为解决经典LM在大规模网络中扩展性瓶颈的有效方案，在回归和分类任务中值得作为一阶优化算法的替代选择进行考虑。

Abstract: The Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) algorithm is a well-known second-order method for rapid convergence and strong robustness when training small- to medium-sized neural networks (NNs). However, its computational and memory costs increase significantly as the number of parameters in an NN grows. To address this limitation, subspace methods have been proposed, such as the Krylov subspace LM (KSLM) and the hybrid subspace LM (HSLM), making second-order algorithms more efficient. In this work, we evaluate the subspace Levenberg-Marquardt algorithms for regression and classification tasks in neural networks. We compare the performance of subspace LM variants with the classical LM method, as well as other popular first-order algorithms, such as stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and Adam.

</details>


### [219] [HarnessEvolve: Learning from Reference Trajectories for Reliable Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00829)
*Wen Jiang,Mingmin Chu,Yimeng Tian,Qianxin Zhang,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Yang Liu,Tao Lv,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessEvolve，一种自进化智能体框架，通过从参考轨迹中学习来克服自进化三大挑战（信用分配失败、捷径学习、灾难性遗忘），实现可靠的harness（提示词、技能、工具、执行逻辑）优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体通过环境反馈优化自身harness以实现更高程度的自主性，但面临三个关键挑战：其一，终态成功/失败反馈导致信用分配失败，难以定位具体出错步骤；其二，智能体容易陷入捷径学习，记忆任务特定模式而非习得可泛化能力；其三，无防护的更新会导致灾难性遗忘，损害已有能力。这些挑战阻碍了可靠的自进化智能体发展。

Method: HarnessEvolve将执行智能体与进化管线解耦，将执行、评估、优化和门控分配给独立智能体模块。具体地：(1) 通过给定ground-truth答案生成参考轨迹，并将失败执行与参考轨迹对齐以提取错误信号，再对错误信号聚类以揭示系统性失败模式，从而解决信用分配失败问题；(2) 设置双重门控机制——质量门过滤数据泄漏和提示膨胀，性能门要求每次更新在当前批次上改善且不退化近期批次性能，并在epoch结束时通过held-out集验证选择最优的快照，以防止捷径学习和灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在覆盖开放域和企业场景的多个基准上，使用不同模型和智能体框架进行的大量实验表明，HarnessEvolve在所有基准和设置下均一致优于最先进的基线方法，验证了跨任务领域的可靠性。

Conclusion: HarnessEvolve通过参考轨迹对齐与双重门控机制，有效解决了自进化过程中信用分配、捷径学习与灾难性遗忘等核心难题，为可靠的自进化智能体提供了可行的框架设计，并在多种任务域和框架下表现出稳定的性能优势。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents advance toward autonomy by optimizing their harness---prompts, skills, tools, and execution logic---based on environmental feedback. This paradigm, however, is hampered by three challenges: \textit{credit assignment failure}, where terminal success/failure feedback makes it ambiguous which step caused the error; \textit{shortcut learning}, where agents memorize task-specific patterns rather than acquire generalizable capabilities; and \textit{catastrophic forgetting}, where unguarded updates degrade previously acquired competence. In this paper, we introduce HarnessEvolve, a self-evolving framework that learns from reference trajectories to achieve reliable agent self-evolution. HarnessEvolve decouples the execution agent from the evolutionary pipeline, assigning execution, evaluation, optimization, and gating to independent agent modules, enabling generalizable and stable harness improvements. Specifically, HarnessEvolve overcomes credit assignment failure by generating reference trajectories (execution paths produced when given the ground-truth answers) and aligning failed executions against them to extract error signals, which are clustered to reveal systematic failure patterns. To prevent shortcut learning and catastrophic forgetting, candidate harness updates must pass two gates: a quality gate that filters data leakage and prompt bloat, and a performance gate that accepts each update if it improves on the current batch without degrading recent batches, with epoch-end validation on a held-out set selecting the best-performing accepted agent snapshot. We conduct extensive experiments on several benchmarks spanning open-domain and enterprise scenarios, using different models and agent frameworks. Results demonstrate that HarnessEvolve consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all benchmarks and settings, confirming reliability across task domains.

</details>


### [220] [Conditional Flow Matching for ML-Based Inverse Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00863)
*Juliana Felder,Milad Habibi,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将条件流匹配（CFM）引入EngiOpt框架，并在结构（beams2d）和热传导（heatconduction2d）两个EngiBench基准任务上与条件扩散模型和条件GAN进行对比。结果显示CFM在两个任务上均取得最低的COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差，且推理速度远快于扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计受限于PDE约束优化问题中迭代求解器的高计算成本及其对初始化的敏感性。深度生成模型有望在不重跑模拟器的情况下生成候选设计，其中GAN单次前向即可采样，而扩散模型需要迭代反向积分，效率较低。

Method: 将条件流匹配（CFM）添加到EngiOpt框架中，并与条件扩散模型和条件GAN在相同下游优化协议下进行比较。使用累积最优性差距（COG）和最终最优性差距（FOG）作为主要评估指标，衡量生成设计作为梯度细化热启动的质量，同时报告MMD和体积分数偏差。

Result: 在两个EngiBench任务上，CFM均获得最低的COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差。CFM的体积分数偏差分别为0.4%（beams2d）和1.0%（heatconduction2d），远优于扩散模型的3.8%和11.2%。在Euler步数s=16时，CFM在beams2d上达到53.2样本/秒，约为扩散基线（1000次网络评估）吞吐量的66倍。CFM生成的初始点比两种基线产生更低的COG，且比扩散模型使用更少的网络评估。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配在生成工程设计的优质热启动方面优于条件扩散模型和条件GAN，兼顾了解的最优性与采样效率，为工程逆向设计中的生成式热启动提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Engineering inverse design is often limited by the high computational cost of iterative solvers for optimization problems constrained by partial differential equations (PDEs) and by their sensitivity to initialization. Deep generative models can produce candidate designs without rerunning the simulator at inference time. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) sample in one forward pass, whereas diffusion models require iterative reverse-time integration.
  In this work, we add conditional flow matching (CFM) to EngiOpt and compare it with a conditional diffusion model and a conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) on structural (beams2d) and thermal (heatconduction2d) benchmarks from EngiBench using the same downstream optimization protocol. We use cumulative optimality gap (COG) and final optimality gap (FOG) as the primary metrics for evaluating the generated designs as warm starts for gradient-based refinement. On the evaluated EngiOpt implementations and two EngiBench tasks, CFM achieves the lowest measured COG, FOG, maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and volume-fraction deviation on both tasks. CFM has mean volume-fraction deviations of 0.4% and 1.0% on beams2d and heatconduction2d, respectively, compared with 3.8% and 11.2% for diffusion. At Euler s = 16, CFM achieves 53.2 samples/s on beams2d, about 66 times the measured throughput of the evaluated diffusion baseline using 1000 network evaluations under the same timing protocol, with COG 1.182 +/- 3.126, compared with 1.173 +/- 3.100 for Euler s = 32.
  Across the two tasks, CFM produces warm starts with lower measured COG than both baselines and uses fewer network evaluations than diffusion.

</details>


### [221] [MemoryWalker: Stop Training Agents on Contexts They Never Saw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00865)
*Zinco J,Xunjie Zhu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Pengjun Xie,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在生产智能体框架（如Claude Code、Qwen-Agent）的压缩式训练中，token驱逐导致有效历史形成树状而非序列，从而引发条件化问题。作者提出两种精确的梯度等价修正方法（LogitTree分段K前向遍历和打包4D注意力掩码），以及一种单次反向传播的变分松弛方法SDCC（自蒸馏用于条件一致性），在七个网络搜索基准上验证了这些方法能显著缩小训练-部署差距并提升rollout奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产智能体harness（如Claude Code和Qwen-Agent）在rollout过程中会压缩上下文，但在这种压缩条件下训练会产生条件化问题：每次驱逐（eviction）都会使有效历史产生分支，导致学习目标是树而非序列。现有的线性化方法要么保留最右路径（造成时间穿越泄漏），要么重放深度优先遍历（造成训练-推理不匹配），均无法正确解决该问题。

Method: 作者提出三类解决方案：1) LogitTree——一种分段K前向遍历方法，需要K+1次反向传播；2) 打包4D注意力掩码——需要自定义kernel和白盒驱逐记录；3) SDCC（Self-Distillation for Conditioning Consistency）——一种单次反向传播的变分松弛方法，在每次驱逐时最小化压缩学生模型与停止梯度的教师模型在前向重建预驱逐前缀上的前向KL散度。

Result: 在包含TC-RAG、AgentFold、MemexRL、Claude Code和OpenCode的七个网络搜索基准上，朴素训练会显著放大训练-rollout对数概率差距，尤其在驱逐密集的批次上。精确方法保持在无压缩基线上，而SDCC大幅缩小差距，具有更低的logit漂移和更高的rollout奖励。残余的每节点KL epsilon_KL给出了训练-部署全变差差距的O(sqrt(epsilon_KL))界。

Conclusion: 压缩训练中的树状条件化问题是导致训练-推理差距的重要来源。精确方法（LogitTree和4D掩码）虽可行但计算开销大或需自定义硬件支持；SDCC作为一个实用的单反向传播变分松弛，在保持效率的同时显著缩小了训练-部署差距，且适用于黑盒harness，具有理论保障。

Abstract: Production agent harnesses such as Claude Code and Qwen-Agent compress context during rollout, but training under compression creates a conditioning problem: every eviction branches the effective history, so the learning object is a tree rather than a sequence. Existing linearizations either retain the rightmost path, causing time-travel leakage, or replay a depth-first traversal, causing train-inference mismatch. We introduce two exact, gradient-equivalent corrections: LogitTree, a segmented K-forward traversal, and a packed 4D attention mask. LogitTree requires K+1 backward passes; the 4D mask requires a custom kernel and white-box eviction records. We also propose SDCC (Self-Distillation for Conditioning Consistency), a single-backward-pass variational relaxation. At each eviction, it minimizes forward KL between the compressed student and a stop-gradient teacher on the reconstructed pre-eviction prefix. A residual per-junction KL of epsilon_KL gives an O(sqrt(epsilon_KL)) bound on the train-deployment total-variation gap. SDCC also applies to black-box harnesses. On seven web-search benchmarks with TC-RAG, AgentFold, MemexRL, Claude Code, and OpenCode, naive training inflates the train-rollout log-probability gap, especially on eviction-heavy batches. The exact methods stay at the no-compression floor, and SDCC substantially closes the gap, with lower logit drift and higher rollout rewards.

</details>


### [222] [iPINN for Broadband CARS Phase Retrieval: A Framework for Function Approximation and Inverse Modeling Problems in Nonlinear Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00883)
*Ravi Teja Vulchi,Carl Messerschmidt,Mohammadsadegh Vafaeinezhad,Rajendhar Junjuri,Tobias Meyer-Zedler,Juergen Popp,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆物理信息神经网络（iPINN），用于宽带相干反斯托克斯拉曼光谱（BCARS）中的相位恢复问题。iPINN结合变压器编码器和可微分的解析前向模型，从原始BCARS光谱预测洛伦兹峰参数并重建共振磁化率，通过多视图一致性损失确保对不同非共振背景模式和噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCARS中的相位恢复是一个不适定逆问题。拉曼信号嵌入在共振磁化率的虚部中，与随采集变化而不同的非共振背景（NRB）相干混合。传统的直接光谱回归方法在不同采集条件下精度下降，因此需要一种对NRB模式、强度和噪声变化具有鲁棒性的相位恢复方法。

Method: 提出iPINN架构，包含：(1) 变压器编码器将光谱特征分配到24个可学习的峰槽（peak slots）；(2) 预测洛伦兹峰参数；(3) 通过可微分的解析前向模型重建共振磁化率；(4) 引入多视图一致性损失，强制模型在不同NRB模式、NRB强度和噪声条件下的预测不变性。

Result: 在公开基准上，iPINN在所有测试基线中取得最低误差（MAE 0.016 vs. 次优0.046）。在跨7种溶剂和4个焦平面位置的28个零样本测试光谱上，5/7的溶剂中精度与深度无关。

Conclusion: 逆参数预测结合可微分物理解码器能够在不同测量条件下实现鲁棒的相位恢复，克服了直接光谱回归对采集条件变化敏感的问题。

Abstract: Phase retrieval in broadband coherent anti-Stokes Raman spectroscopy (BCARS) is an ill-posed inverse problem. The Raman-like signal is encoded in the imaginary part of the resonant susceptibility, which mixes coherently with a non-resonant background (NRB) that varies across acquisitions. We introduce an inverse physics-informed neural network (iPINN) that predicts Lorentzian peak parameters from raw BCARS spectra and reconstructs the resonant susceptibility through a differentiable analytical forward model. A transformer encoder assigns spectral features to 24 learnable peak slots, and a multi-view consistency loss enforces invariance across NRB pattern, NRB strength, and noise. Unlike direct spectral regression approaches, the method retains accuracy under varying acquisition conditions. On a public benchmark, iPINN achieves the lowest error among the tested baselines (MAE 0.016 vs. next-best 0.046). On 28 zero-shot test spectra acquired across seven solvents and four focal positions, accuracy is depth-invariant in five of seven solvents. These results show that inverse parametric prediction with a differentiable physical decoder supports robust phase retrieval across measurement conditions.

</details>


### [223] [Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems with Time-varying Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00896)
*Jiahao Wang,Yijun Wang,Nan Fang,Sikun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有时变转移核的泊松-伽马动态系统（TV-PGDS），通过三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链（Dir-Dir、Dir-Gam-Dir、PR-Gam-Dir）建模转移矩阵随时间演化的特性，并利用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增强技术构建了全共轭高效的Gibbs采样器进行后验推断，实验表明其在计数时间序列预测上优于相关基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的泊松-伽马动态系统（PGDS）虽然能够有效捕捉计数时间序列的潜在动态结构，但其转移矩阵是静态的，无法刻画真实世界计数时间序列中常见的时变转移动态（即潜在状态之间的依赖结构随时间发生结构性突变）。

Method: 提出TV-PGDS模型，允许底层转移矩阵随时间演化。设计了三种基于Dirichlet分布的马尔可夫链（Dir-Dir、Dir-Gam-Dir、PR-Gam-Dir）来适应依赖结构中的异质性突变。采用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增强技术，推导出全共轭且高效的Gibbs采样器进行后验模拟。

Result: 与相关模型相比，TV-PGDS通过学习时变转移矩阵所捕捉的动态依赖结构，获得了更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: TV-PGDS有效克服了现有PGDS在时变转移动态建模上的不足，提升了计数时间序列的预测能力，为贝叶斯计数时间序列建模提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Bayesian methodologies for handling count-valued time series have gained prominence due to their ability to infer interpretable latent structures and to estimate uncertainties. Among these Bayesian models, Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems (PGDSs) are proven to be effective in capturing the evolving dynamics underlying observed count sequences. However, the state-of-the-art PGDS still falls short in capturing the transition dynamics that are commonly observed in real-world count time series. To mitigate this limitation, a PGDS with time-varying transition kernel (TV-PGDS), is proposed to allow the underlying transition matrices to evolve over time. Three specifically-designed Dirichlet Markov chains (Dir-Dir, Dir-Gam-Dir, PR-Gam-Dir) are constructed to accommodate heterogeneous structural mutations within these dependencies. Leveraging Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta data augmentation techniques, a fully-conjugate and efficient Gibbs sampler is developed to perform posterior simulation. Experiments show that, in comparison with related models, the proposed PGDS achieves improved predictive performance due to its capacity to learn time-varying dependency structure captured by the time-evolving transition matrices.

</details>


### [224] [When Metropolis and Hastings Meet Bradley and Terry: Exact MCMC From Preference Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00905)
*Ariel Smogorghevski,Nir Rosenfeld,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pref-MH是一种通用的精确Metropolis-Hastings采样器，仅利用随机二元成对比较即可对由"评判者"（judge）诱导的条件分布进行采样，在文本生成、分子设计和图像生成等领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型中从满足特定语义属性的条件分布中采样是一个新兴挑战。MH方法需要精确的逐点目标密度评估，这在生成式设置中不可用；而人类或模型评判者提供的成对比较极易获取且已证明价值广泛，因此需要一个仅基于二元偏好反馈即可实现精确条件采样的框架。

Method: Pref-MH的关键观察在于：MH的未归一化密度比恰好对应Bradley-Terry（BT）选择模型中的偏好几率（preference odds）。由于MH需要精确的比率计算而BT评判者只提供采样的二元反馈，作者设计了一个有效的接受/拒绝规则，使得所得马尔可夫链可证明地收敛到目标分布。此外，对于固定的提议核与预算，Pref-MH在Peskun-Tierney意义下是此类精确可逆接受规则中的最优方案。

Result: 在LLM评判者驱动的文本生成与分子设计实验，以及VLM评判者驱动的图像生成实验中，Pref-MH展示了在比较式反馈相对容易获取时，作为一种实用且灵活的条件采样方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Pref-MH填补了生成式条件采样中"只有二元偏好比较、无精确密度评估"的空白，通过将MH的密度比与BT偏好几率巧妙关联，实现了可证明收敛且最优的精确采样算法，为条件生成提供了一种通用且实用的工具。

Abstract: Sampling from distributions conditioned on desired semantic properties is an emerging challenge in modern generative modeling. Metropolis-Hastings (MH) provides a principled route to conditional sampling, but requires access to exact pointwise target-density evaluations, which are not available in generative settings. Meanwhile, pairwise comparisons by humans or model "judge" are highly accessible and have proved valuable across diverse applications. We introduce Pref-MH, a general exact MH sampler for judge-induced conditional distributions using only stochastic binary pairwise comparisons. Our key observation is that the MH unnormalized density ratio matches the preference odds of the Bradley-Terry (BT) choice model. The central challenge is that while MH requires precise ratio computation, BT judges provide only sampled binary feedback. To this end, we develop a valid accept/reject rule whose resulting Markov chain provably converges to the target distribution. We further show that, for a fixed proposal kernel and budget, Pref-MH is optimal in the Peskun-Tierney sense among this class of exact reversible acceptance rules. Experiments on text generation and molecular design with LLM judges, as well as image generation with VLM judges, demonstrate that Pref-MH provides a practical and flexible approach to conditional sampling when comparative feedback is relatively easy to obtain.

</details>


### [225] [The Multiple Timescales of Gradient Descent on the Edge of Stability: A Perturbative Derivation of the Central Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01034)
*Raphaël Berthier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个微扰框架，将Cohen等人(2025)提出的深度学习中梯度下降在稳定性边界的连续时间模型（中心流）严格化。通过假设损失函数分解为f=g+εh，在ε→0的极限下，梯度下降动力学收敛到在g的极小值流形上、锐度不超过2/η约束下的h的梯度流。利用奇异摄动理论中的多重尺度方法，作者推导出ε展开：中心流作为领头阶项涌现，自稳定化机制出现在次阶项中，并进一步分析了单个及多个特征值位于稳定性边界时的自稳定化动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cohen等人(2025)提出一个刻画深度学习在稳定性边界（edge of stability）梯度下降行为的连续时间模型——中心流，该模型经验上准确但推导是启发式的，缺乏严格的数学基础。本文旨在为该模型提供一个可解析的微扰理论框架，从梯度下降动力学中严格推导出中心流，从而填补理论与实证之间的gap。

Method: 本文采用奇异摄动理论的方法。核心假设是将损失函数分解为f=g+εh，其中ε→0表示微扰极限。将梯度下降视为ε的奇异摄动动力系统，识别出三个时间尺度：(1)沿最锐方向的快速振荡；(2)自稳定化机制的中间时间尺度；(3)沿g极小值流形运动的慢时间尺度（即中心流）。利用多重尺度方法（method of multiple scales）对动力学进行ε展开，推导出各阶近似。

Result: 作者推导出中心流作为展开的领头阶项涌现，自稳定化机制出现在次阶项中。对于单一特征值位于稳定性边界的情况，计算了涨落能量的慢漂移；对于多个特征值位于稳定性边界的情况，推导出自稳定化系统，并解释了涨落为何持续存在而不衰减。

Conclusion: 本文通过奇异摄动分析为Cohen等人(2025)的中心流提供了形式化的推导框架，将中心流解释为梯度下降在特定微扰极限下的领头阶行为。这深化了对深度学习优化中自稳定化机制的理解，尤其揭示了多特征值场景下涨落持续性背后的动力学原理。该框架虽是形式化的而非严格的，但为后续严格分析提供了方向。

Abstract: The central flow of Cohen et al. (2025) is an empirically accurate continuous-time model of gradient descent at the edge of stability in deep learning, However, its derivation is heuristic. We propose a perturbative regime in which the central flow is the limit of gradient descent: we assume that the loss decomposes as $f = g + \varepsilon h$; in the limit $\varepsilon \to 0$, the dynamics of gradient descent with learning rate $η$ converge to the gradient flow of $h$ constrained to the minimizers of $g$ of sharpness at most $2/η$. Our approach is formal rather than rigorous; it treats gradient descent as a singularly perturbed dynamical system in $\varepsilon$. Three timescales emerge: a fast timescale of oscillations along the sharpest direction, an intermediate timescale of the self-stabilization mechanism, and a slow timescale of the dynamics along the minimizers of $g$-the central flow. Using the method of multiple scales, a classical formal method from singular perturbation theory, we derive the expansion of the dynamics in $\varepsilon$: the central flow emerges as the leading-order term in the expansion, while the self-stabilization mechanism appears in the next-order term. We study this mechanism beyond previous analyses: with a single eigenvalue at the edge of stability, we compute the slow drift of the energy of the fluctuations; with several eigenvalues at the edge of stability, we derive the self-stabilization system and explain why fluctuations persist.

</details>


### [226] [From Truncation to Commitment: Persistent Context in Uniform Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01043)
*Satoshi Hayakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出committed reveal sampling（CRS），一种无需训练的采样方法，用于统一状态离散扩散模型。CRS存储argmax选择的token并将其作为持久上下文插入后续模型输入，不同于传统方法中每个位置的预测仅影响当前反向步骤。实验表明，与固定top-p截断基线相比，CRS在相同最终化规则下可获得更低的生成困惑度（GenPPL），并实现更有利的GenPPL-熵权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一状态离散扩散模型以并行方式更新所有token且每个位置均可反复修订。即使top-p规则在某位置只剩一个候选时，该选择也只影响当前反向步骤，可在下一步被修订。作者质疑：若选定的假设变成持久的上下文用于后续预测，情况会如何变化？即探讨是否存在一种更好的采样策略，将已选token"承诺"并注入后续模型输入。

Method: CRS是一种训练免费的采样器，存储选定的argmax token并将其插入后续模型输入中。理论分析提供了两个理由：(1)从精确前向过程看，随噪声减小，选择干净token的贝叶斯误差不会增加，支持"晚选择"；(2)在简单隐模式模型中，保持选定token可见有助于后续并行预测达成一致的序列级选择。实验上使用Duo蒸馏模型进行配对实验，将CRS的持久化效应与单步top-p限制及标量温度缩放分离。

Result: 在相同最终化规则下，不使用top-p截断的CRS在8-64次函数评估（NFE）预算下比固定p=0.95和p=0.9基线达到更低的GenPPL。在64 NFE下，在匹配的unigram熵对比中CRS仍获得更低GenPPL，呈现更有利的GenPPL-熵权衡。Base Duo在描述性比较中显示相同方向，但其他多样性和延续性指标对这些操作点的排序可能不同。

Conclusion: 实验结果识别出"支持限制"和"持久上下文"是GenPPL-熵权衡的两种独立控制手段。CRS通过持久化已选token的上下文来实现更优的采样效果，为离散扩散模型的采样策略提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Uniform-state discrete diffusion models update all tokens in parallel while keeping every position revisable. Even when the commonly used top-$p$ rule leaves only one candidate at a position, that choice affects only the current reverse step and can be revised at the next sampling step. We ask what changes when selected hypotheses instead become persistent context for later predictions. We therefore propose committed reveal sampling (CRS), a training-free sampler that stores selected argmax tokens and inserts them into subsequent model inputs. Our analysis gives a rationale for selecting later and for keeping selected tokens visible. Under the exact forward process, the Bayes error of selecting a clean token cannot increase as noise decreases, while in a simple latent-mode model, keeping the selected token visible helps later parallel predictions agree on the same sequence-level choice. Empirically, paired experiments on Duo-distilled then separate this persistent effect from single-step top-$p$ restriction and scalar temperature scaling. Under the same finalization rule, CRS without top-$p$ truncation reaches lower generative perplexity (GenPPL) than fixed $p=0.95$ and $p=0.9$ baselines across budgets of 8--64 function evaluations (NFE). At 64 NFE, the comparison at matched unigram entropy also gives lower GenPPL for CRS, yielding a more favorable GenPPL--entropy tradeoff. Base Duo shows the same direction in a descriptive comparison, while other diversity and continuation metrics can rank these operating points differently. These results identify support restriction and persistent context as distinct controls of that tradeoff.

</details>


### [227] [SAGE: Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement for Mitigating Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01051)
*Yiming Luo,Rongqiang Zhao,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（子群体感知生成增强），一种无需群体标签的两阶段生成式增强框架，通过聚类生成子标签并结合类标签微调条件生成模型和文本编码器，生成针对性合成数据填补训练集中未被充分覆盖的区域，并构建平衡验证集用于最后一层重加权，从而缓解机器学习中的虚假相关性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集固有分布不平衡导致传统ERM模型依赖多数群体的虚假属性进行分类，在少数群体上表现差。当虚假属性（群体标签）不可用时，现有无标签方法常通过重复上采样少数群体或误分类的真实样本，这会降低有效多样性并导致过拟合。因此需要一种数据层面的生成式增强方法，在无先验知识条件下缓解虚假相关性。

Method: SAGE采用两阶段生成式增强框架：第一阶段利用聚类派生的子标签和类标签来微调条件生成模型及文本编码器；第二阶段生成针对性的合成数据以填补训练集中代表性不足的区域，同时构建平衡的验证集用于最后一层的重加权（last-layer reweighting）。

Result: 在Waterbirds、CelebA和MetaShift基准上，SAGE分别达到89.5%、85.7%和79.1%的最差群体准确率，相比最佳的无群体标签基线方法最多提升7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了从数据视角出发，利用生成式方法在无群体标签场景下可以有效缓解虚假相关性，优于现有无标签订单方法，同时避免了重复上采样带来的过拟合问题。

Abstract: Spurious correlations pose a significant challenge to the robustness of modern machine learning. The inherent imbalance in dataset distributions often leads traditional Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) models to rely on majority spurious attributes for classification, resulting in poor performance on minority groups. This problem becomes particularly challenging when the spurious attributes are unavailable. Existing group-label-free methods often upsample minority groups or misclassified real training examples; repeating the same instances can reduce effective diversity and encourage overfitting. To mitigate these spurious correlations from a data-centric perspective in the absence of prior knowledge, we introduce Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement (SAGE), a two-stage generative augmentation framework. Using cluster-derived sub-labels and class labels, we fine-tune a conditional generative model and text encoder, generating targeted synthetic data to fill underrepresented regions in the training set and construct a balanced validation set for last-layer reweighting. We experimentally show that SAGE achieves 89.5%, 85.7%, and 79.1% worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds, CelebA, and MetaShift, respectively, outperforming the best group-label-free baselines by up to 7.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [228] [Let Confidence Change, Not the Prediction: Prediction-Preserving Repair for Post-hoc Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01072)
*Daehwan Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORD 是一个后验适配器，通过修复完整校准概率向量，在保持原始 top-1 预测不变的前提下实现精确的概率校准，同时在多个数据集上降低 ECE、NLL 和 Brier 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后校准虽然能修正模型报告的置信度，但多类校准器可能改变原先的 top-1 预测类别，而准确率只反映这种变化的净效应，无法刻画预测改变频率。现有校准方法缺乏在保持 top-1 决策不变的同时进行校准的手段。

Method: CORD 从原始输出与校准输出中，确定分配给原始 top-1 类别的质量；随后将剩余质量按校准后的条件分布分配给其他类别，从而构建一个其 argmax 恢复原始预测的修复向量。该适配器不改变已拟合的校准器及其直接输出，不拟合额外的有监督映射，也不需要用户或验证集调节的超参数。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100 与 ImageNet-1K 上，CORD 构造性地实现零 TPCR，并在所有数据集中相对直接校准输出降低了平均 ECE、NLL 与 Brier；配对收益在分布偏移和不同校准集规模下保持稳定。

Conclusion: CORD 将预测保持的约束从校准器拟合过程中移除，并将其转化为对校准输出的后处理修复任务，从而在不同应用中能精确恢复原始 top-1 决策，同时保留校准的效益。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration corrects reported confidence, yet a multiclass calibrator can also change the associated top-1 prediction. Accuracy captures only the net effect of these changes on correctness, not how often predictions change; the Top-1 Prediction Change Rate (TPCR) instead measures this frequency. We propose Calibrator-Output Repair for Top-1 Decision Preservation (CORD), the first post-fit adapter to impose exact prediction preservation by repairing the full calibrated probability vector. From the original and calibrated outputs alone, CORD determines the mass assigned to the original top-1. The calibrated conditional distribution allocates the remaining mass over the other classes, yielding a repaired vector whose own argmax recovers the original prediction. On the calibration split, CORD coordinates the repaired masses to retain the calibrated outputs' mean mass on original predictions whenever attainable. The adapter alters neither the fitted calibrator nor its direct output, fits no additional supervised map, and requires no user- or validation-tuned hyperparameter. Across CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet-1K, CORD attains zero TPCR by construction and lowers mean ECE, NLL, and Brier relative to the corresponding direct outputs in every dataset; paired gains persist under distribution shift and across calibration-set sizes. CORD thus removes the preservation constraint from calibrator fitting and assigns exact recovery of the original decision to subsequent output repair. Our code is available at https://github.com/labhai/ORCU.

</details>


### [229] [Modelpedia: A Catalog of Model Findings for the Meta-Science of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01090)
*Franciszek Bernat,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Luca Longo,Jianlong Zhou,Andreas Holzinger,Riccardo Guidotti,Wojciech Samek,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Modelpedia，一个利用LLM自动化从论文中提取模型相关研究发现，并将其关联到模型、数据集、方法和概念，聚合成可搜索公共目录的框架。原型应用于ICLR 2024和2025论文，提取了上千条发现，并基于目录对社区研究AI模型的方式进行了元分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于AI模型的科学知识生产速度快于社区整理消化它们的速度，新基础模型频繁重塑领域，大量论文、博客和技术报告记录了模型的行为与失败，但这些发现彼此分散且实际上不可检索。亟需一个系统化的框架来提取、组织和检索这类知识。

Method: Modelpedia是一个自动化的、LLM辅助的框架，用于：1) 从发表的论文中提取关于模型的研究发现；2) 将发现链接到其所涉及的模型、数据集、方法和概念；3) 将结果聚合成一个可搜索的公共目录。作者将该原型应用于ICLR 2024和2025被接收论文进行验证。

Result: 在ICLR 2024和2025论文中提取了超过一千条研究发现，并将目录本身作为研究对象，对社区如何研究AI模型进行了元分析。

Conclusion: 作者邀请社区探索、贡献并基于该开放目录进行构建，帮助将模型研究发现确立为AI元科学的共享基础。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge about AI models is produced faster than the community can organize it. Every few months a new foundation model reshapes the field and hundreds of papers, blogs, and technical reports document how each behaves or fails. Yet, these findings remain scattered and effectively unretrievable. To address this gap we present Modelpedia, an automated, LLM-assisted framework that extracts findings about models from published papers, links it to the model, dataset, method, and concept it concerns, and aggregates the result into a searchable public catalog. Applying the prototype to accepted ICLR 2024 and 2025 papers, we extract over a thousand findings and, treating the catalog itself as an object of study, run a meta-analysis of how the community investigates models. Now, we invite the community to explore, contribute to, and build on the open catalog, and to help establish model findings as a shared foundation for the meta-science of AI.

</details>


### [230] [Subliminal Learning as Trait-Direction Drift: A Mechanism and Targeted Control under SFT Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01091)
*Zhixuan Liu,Zhichen Dong,Yuyu Fan,Xiangtian Li,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了"特质方向漂移"（trait-direction drift）作为知识蒸馏中隐性学习（subliminal learning）的机制，并提出探测空间走廊正则化（probe-space corridor regularization）作为针对性防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏可以将教师的隐藏特质意外传递给下游学生模型。虽然已有研究发现了蒸馏中隐性学习的多个环节，但信号如何在训练过程中累积并产生行为迁移仍不清楚，导致难以进行针对性缓解。

Method: 提出特质方向漂移机制：教师偏见生成造成数据中的可测量偏好差距，学生模型在监督微调中识别这些差距并产生特质对齐更新，逐步累积为行为迁移。基于此机制，提出探测空间走廊正则化，在蒸馏过程中约束模型沿校准后的特质方向的漂移。

Result: 该方法大幅降低隐藏特质迁移的同时保持任务性能：恶意响应迁移从29.55%降至6.45%，且主任务准确率损失很低；在Qwen设置下持续抑制动物偏好迁移。偏好差距、训练轨迹和干预实验证据均支持特质方向漂移机制。

Conclusion: 偏好差距、训练轨迹与干预三类证据将隐性学习与特质方向漂移机制联系起来，走廊正则化可作为蒸馏过程中针对性的隐特质控制手段。

Abstract: Beyond intended capabilities, model distillation can transfer hidden traits from a teacher. A teacher biased by a system prompt can generate semantically clean training data, such as numeric sequences, that still causes a downstream student to inherit the hidden preference, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. Prior work has identified several parts of this process. How the signal builds up during training and produces behavioral transfer remains unclear, making targeted mitigation difficult. We propose and validate trait-direction drift as a mechanism for subliminal learning: biased generation creates measurable preference gaps in teacher data, and student-recognizable gaps induce trait-aligned updates during supervised fine-tuning that accumulate into behavioral transfer. Guided by this mechanism, we propose probe-space corridor regularization, a targeted defense that constrains drift along a calibrated trait direction during distillation. The method substantially reduces hidden-trait transfer, preserving task performance: for example, it lowers malicious-response transfer from 29.55% to 6.45% with low main-task accuracy cost, and consistently suppresses animal-preference transfer across the main Qwen setting. The preference-gap, training-trajectory, and intervention evidence links subliminal learning to trait-direction drift and motivates corridor regularization as a targeted control during distillation.

</details>


### [231] [Neural Symbollic Regression Using Deep Learning and Sparse Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01102)
*Ravi Kumar U,Sumitra S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号回归（NSR）框架，将神经网络作为函数预处理器，结合LASSO稀疏回归从数据中发现简洁、可解释的数学表达式。实验证明该方法在Nguyen基准上优于SINDy和未调优的神经基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归方法（如遗传编程）存在可扩展性差、对噪声敏感的问题，而稀疏回归方法（如SINDy）依赖预定义特征库。现有方法难以兼顾可扩展性、噪声鲁棒性和符号发现的解释性，需要一种更灵活且可扩展的框架来缩小神经逼近与稀疏方程发现之间的差距。

Method: 采用解耦流水线：1) 神经网络在交互感知的非线性特征空间中学习目标函数的光滑、噪声鲁棒的近似；2) 通过LASSO提取稀疏、可解释的闭式表达式；3) 集成Ray Tune和ASHA调度的分布式超参数优化以提升预测精度和符号保真度。

Result: 在Nguyen基准套件上的实验表明，该方法在RMSE、噪声鲁棒性和分布外泛化方面一致优于SINDy和未调优的神经基线。消融研究确认了特征交互、神经深度和调优策略的重要性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一个可扩展且可理解的神经-符号框架，为科学机器学习在神经逼近与稀疏方程发现之间建立了坚实的桥梁。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) seeks to find succinct mathematical expressions that represent the fundamental relationships within data, providing interpretability and scientific understanding that exceeds that of black-box models. Nevertheless, traditional methods like Genetic Programming face challenges with scalability and are highly sensitive to noise, while sparse regression techniques such as SINDy rely significantly on predetermined feature libraries. In this work, we present a Neural Symbolic Regression (NSR) framework that treats neural networks as functional preconditioners for symbolic discovery. Our approach uses a decoupled pipeline: a neural network first learns a smooth, noise-robust approximation of the target function in an interaction- aware nonlinear feature space. LASSO is then applied to extract sparse, interpretable closed-form expressions. To improve predictive accuracy and symbolic fidelity by integrating distributed hyperparameter optimization with Ray Tune and ASHA scheduling. Experiments on the Nguyen benchmark suite show that our approach consistently outperforms SINDy and non-tuned neural baselines in RMSE, noise robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization. Ablation studies confirm the significance of feature interactions, neural depth, and tuning strategies. In general, this study presents a scalable and understandable neural-symbolic framework, creating a solid link between neural approximation and the discovery of sparse equations for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [232] [Replicating TRACE: A Practitioner's Guide to Its Threshold and Particle Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01108)
*Alex Chadyuk,Alicia Zhang,Roy Kucukates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了TRACE方法在合成基准上的核心结果（词表1000时平均逐序列F1达0.90-0.91），但通过多角度剖析揭示了其潜在局限：最优阈值依赖真值边距而非固定常数；单全局阈值下TRACE主要恢复一阶（滞后1）直接边，长滞后边召回极低；基准默认的滞后衰减将约85%干预真值集中于滞后1，使头条F1仅证明滞后1恢复能力；F1饱和源于阈值对噪声底限的边距而非估计器的统计性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并深入剖析TRACE从预训练自回归序列模型中读取因果图的可靠性边界，揭示其头条性能指标背后隐藏的真值分布偏斜、阈值敏感性和滞后分辨力等系统性局限，为实践者提供可操作的准则。

Method: 独立复现TRACE在合成基准上的实验（词表大小100-2000、N=2-多粒子），在验证集上挑选阈值，对比逐序列F1与精确干预真值；系统变化滞后衰减斜率、单词表、粒子数等参数，评估最优阈值位置、各滞后召回率、阈值族恢复能力，以及F1饱和的成因。

Result: (1) 复现F1达到0.90-0.91（词表1000）且0.86-0.91（100-2000）；(2) 最优阈值不是常数而由真值delta=0.05边距决定，盲选最优约在delta/2×估计器标定处；(3) 单全局阈值下滞后1真实边召回0.97-0.99，滞后≥2的边召回低若干个数量级，逐滞后阈值族仅恢复滞后2真值的1/3到1/2；(4) 基准默认滞后衰减集中约85%真值于滞后1，扁平衰减才能区分基准偏斜与算法自身极限；(5) F1在N=2即饱和，源于阈值对噪声底限的边距，而估计器本身按N^(-1/2)收敛。据此总结出五条实践准则。

Conclusion: TRACE的头条F1成绩经不起内部分解检验：它主要度量的是滞后1边的恢复能力，且成绩高度依赖基准的滞后分布偏斜与阈值选择；估计器的噪声底限而非估计本身主导了有限样本下的观测饱和。实践者应采用逐滞后阈值族并在扁平滞后衰减下评估，以正确区分算法能力与基准偏斜。

Abstract: TRACE (Math & Lienhart, arXiv:2602.01135) reads causal graphs over event types out of a pretrained autoregressive sequence model by thresholding a per-position conditional-mutual-information estimate at a fixed tau. We independently replicate its headline synthetic result: with tau selected on a validation split, mean per-sequence F1 against exact interventional truth reaches 0.90-0.91 at vocabulary size 1000 (paper: 0.91) and 0.86-0.91 from 100 to 2000. First, the optimal threshold is pinned to the truth margin, not to any constant: at every size the errors at tau* straddle the delta = 0.05 margin defining ground truth (missed true edges lie just above it, accepted false ones just below), and the blind optimum lands near delta/2 times the estimator's calibration, confirmed out of sample at 5000. Second, at a single global threshold TRACE mostly recovers a direct, adjacent-influence graph: lag-1 true edges are recalled at 0.97-0.99, while true edges at lag 2 or more read orders of magnitude lower---the reading-scale price of randomizing mediating positions, which an exact test of direct causal effect requires when the truth is unknown. A per-lag threshold family recovers a third to a half of lag-2 truth; on lag-uniform data one validated threshold recalls every lag at 0.40-0.87, 8-26 pp below an atomic-intervention control at lags 3-6. Third, the default lag decay of the paper's synthetic benchmark concentrates about 85% of interventional truth at lag 1 and pushes the rest below the estimator's noise floor, so headline F1 there certifies lag-1 recovery only and conflates the benchmark's skew with the algorithm's own limit; a flatter decay separates the two. Fourth, F1 saturates from N = 2 particles at the selected threshold---a property of the threshold's margin over the noise floor, not of the estimator, which converges as N^(-1/2). We distill five practitioner rules.

</details>


### [233] [When Does Online Adaptation Pay on the Edge? A Leakage-Free Evaluation of Warmup, Learning-Rate Selection, and Resource Trade-offs for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01126)
*Takumi Fujimoto,Hiroaki Nishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了边缘时间序列预测中在线自适应方法的评估偏差问题，识别出两个新的比较偏差来源——静态基线的预热预算和优化器比较时的学习率混淆，并提出了一种仅依赖验证集的部署程序来公平评估在线自适应收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自适应在边缘时间序列预测中对分布漂移有潜在帮助，但其实际收益严重依赖于评估方式的选择。已有研究可能因评估协议中的偏差而夸大或低估自适应带来的收益，因此需要对评估方法本身进行系统性审查。

Method: 作者在六个公开多变量时间序列数据集（包括建筑传感器和智能电表数据）上，采用无泄漏的流式评估协议。通过改变静态基线的预热预算（1,000到20,000步），使用预漂移验证切片仅选择预热预算和各优化器的在线学习率（不访问测试数据），并将SGD+momentum与Adam在各自最优学习率下进行公平比较。同时评估全量、仅头部和基于校准的自适应方法在精度-状态内存权衡上的表现。

Result: 预热预算对估计的自适应收益影响显著，在六种数据集-骨干网络组合中变化幅度达3.0至18.8个百分点。在验证驱动的最优学习率选择下，Adam在360个评估单元中的310个优于SGD+m，但仍有4个Adam单元低于静态基线。在参数高效变体中，多个方法在状态内存轴上不受支配。智能电表分析显示收益大小取决于电表选择规则。

Conclusion: 研究支持采用仅验证集的部署流程来评估在线自适应方法，为边缘时间序列预测的公平比较提供了实践指引。当前报告的结果中目标设备延迟和能耗尚未测量，是后续工作方向。

Abstract: Online adaptation can help edge time-series forecasting under distribution drift, but its measured benefit is sensitive to evaluation choices. We study six public multivariate streams, including building-sensor and smart-meter data, under a leakage-free streaming protocol. We identify two additional sources of comparison bias. First, the warmup budget of the static baseline has a two-sided effect: insufficient warmup undertrains the baseline, whereas excessive warmup can degrade its pre-drift generalization. Across six dataset-backbone settings, the estimated adaptation benefit changes by 3.0 to 18.8 percentage points (pp) over the 1,000-20,000-step warmup range. Second, comparing SGD with momentum (SGD+m) and Adam at a shared default learning rate conflates optimizer quality with rate sensitivity. We select both the warmup budget and each optimizer's online rate using a held-out pre-drift validation slice without accessing test data. Under this validation-only procedure, Adam outperforms SGD+m in 310 of 360 evaluated cells, while 4 Adam cells remain below the static baseline. We further characterize accuracy against adaptation-state memory and A100-measured per-update latency for full, head-only, and calibration-based adaptation. In the evaluated PatchTST frontier settings, several parameter-efficient variants are nondominated on the adaptation-state-memory axis. Smart-meter analyses also show that reported gains depend on meter-selection rules. These findings support a validation-only commissioning procedure, while target-device latency and energy remain to be measured. Code, data, and all reported numbers: https://github.com/keiotakmin/tsf-edge-adaptation.

</details>


### [234] [Scaled Idempotence in Transformer Attention: Paired OV Geometry and Shared-Value Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01129)
*Jiming Feng,Junliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer注意力中的一种规整代数性质：有效的OV算子T=OV^T的近闭包性表现为T^2≈αT。在六个预训练模型（2.8B-235B参数）中，仅有少量（3.98-8.00%）头达到平方闭包对齐P≥0.9，且无对照的层内O/V不匹配达到该水平。通过精确主坐标分解T=Q_OK Q_V^T和T^2=Q_O(KDK)Q_V^T，将支撑内传输与读写返回几何分离。在九个MHA/GQA模型的7304个头中，随机打乱K方向仅保留奇异值/范数/因子跨度及主角度，可将中位闭包从0.336降至1.04×10^-4；受训方向在98.64%的头及所有层中胜出。在精确值共享下，逐头闭包扩展为右作用代数T_iT_j=α_jT_i。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer注意力中是否存在可识别的代数规律性，特别是OV算子（值与输出投影的组合）在组成近似封闭的结构特性，以揭示注意力机制内部组织与训练的几何/代数本质。

Method: 识别OV算子平方近似闭包规律T^2≈αT；引入精确主坐标分解分离传输与返回几何；通过跨六个规模端点（2.8B-235B）的系统测量、随机方向扰动的对照实验、构造性搜索、多谱系回顾性轨迹分析以及七模型值共享代数验证，全面评估闭包性的来源与泛化。

Result: 跨模型仅少数头（≤8%）达高闭包对齐P≥0.9；主坐标分解显示支撑内传输与读写返回的几何分离；方向是闭包性的关键——随机方向使闭包从0.336骤降至1e-4量级，98.64%头中受训方向占优；每个层均具备高闭包的构造可行性但未必被利用；值共享使逐头闭包扩展为头间右作用代数。

Conclusion: 缩放幂等性（scaled idempotence）是广泛可用几何容量内的一种稀疏受训方向特征，而非架构固有的必然结果；值共享机制进一步将逐头关系延伸为局部算子代数，为理解注意力头的计算组织提供了代数框架。

Abstract: We identify a recurrent algebraic regularity in Transformer attention: a sparse subset of effective OV operators $T=OV^\top$ nearly closes under composition, $T^2\approxαT$. Across six pretrained endpoints spanning 2.8B--235B parameters, 3.98--8.00% of heads reach squared closure alignment $\mathcal{P}\geq0.9$, while no matched within-layer O/V mismatch does. An exact principal-coordinate factorization, $T=Q_OKQ_V^\top$ and $T^2=Q_O(KDK)Q_V^\top$, separates within-support transport from read--write return geometry. Across all 7,304 heads in nine MHA/GQA models, scrambling only the orientation of $K$ while preserving singular values, norms, factor spans, and principal angles reduces median closure from 0.336 to $1.04\times10^{-4}$; trained orientation wins for 98.64% of heads and in every layer. Constructive searches show that high closure is feasible in every surveyed layer, but usually not attained. Retrospective trajectories in three independently trained lineages further separate broadly available capacity from the orientations attained by final strong heads. Under exact value sharing, headwise closure extends to a right-action algebra, $T_iT_j=α_jT_i$. Seven-model experiments verify the approximate law and reveal distinct oblique projections with a shared value-defined kernel. These results characterize scaled idempotence as a sparse trained orientation within broadly available geometric capacity and show how value sharing extends a headwise relation into a local operator algebra.

</details>


### [235] [Superposed Latent Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01158)
*Quanling Zhao,Jiaying Yang,Tianqi Zhang,Ziyang Hao,Fatemeh Asgarinejad,Flavio Ponzina,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超位置潜变量自编码器（SLAE），摒弃传统自编码器通过缩小潜变量维度来节省内存的做法，转而通过"保持潜变量宽维、让其共享存储"的新原则实现表示压缩。SLAE将高容量潜表示转换为存储友好的编码，用随机密钥绑定，将多个编码叠加为单一记忆张量，并学习在解码前恢复每个潜变量。在CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、STL-10、Tiny ImageNet及多种内存预算下，SLAE将重构误差最高降低56%，并将下游分类准确率最多提升16.79个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自编码器在面对紧凑的潜记忆预算时，通常通过缩小每个潜表示的维度（即不可逆的维度瓶颈）来降低存储，但这牺牲了表示容量。作者提出一个不同的问题：是否能将多个更宽的潜变量存储在一起，在保持高容量表示的同时共享存储空间，从而克服窄瓶颈带来的容量损失。

Method: SLAE采用一个多阶段流程：(1) 将潜变量转换为存储友好的编码；(2) 用随机化密钥进行绑定；(3) 将多个编码叠加（superpose）到单一的记忆张量中；(4) 在该叠加表示上学习恢复每个原始潜变量，再进行解码。该方法以结构化干扰取代不可逆的维度压缩，使宽表示在相同存储预算下可被有效利用。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、STL-10、Tiny ImageNet等多个数据集及各种内存预算下，SLAE在匹配存储的条件下将重构误差较传统自编码器降低最高56%；下游分类准确率在同内存预算下最高提升16.79个百分点。分析进一步表明，SLAE的优势源于能在相同存储预算下利用更宽的表示。

Conclusion: SLAE验证了一个新的表示压缩原理：与其把每个潜变量做小，不如保持表示宽泛并通过叠加机制让它们共享内存。该方法在重构与下游任务上均显著优于传统自编码器，为表征压缩提供了一种不同于维度缩减的替代范式。

Abstract: Autoencoders typically meet tight latent-memory budgets by making each latent representation smaller, sacrificing representational capacity. We ask a different question: can multiple wider latents be stored together instead? We introduce the Superposed Latent Autoencoder (SLAE), which preserves high-capacity latent representations while sharing storage through learned superposition. SLAE transforms latents into storage-friendly codes, binds them with randomized keys, superposes multiple codes into a single memory tensor, and learns to recover each latent before decoding. Under the same storage budget, SLAE replaces irreversible dimensional bottlenecks with structured interference that can be suppressed. Across CIFAR-10/100, SVHN, STL-10, Tiny ImageNet, and a wide range of memory budgets, SLAE substantially improves the reconstruction--memory tradeoff, reducing reconstruction error by up to 56% over conventional autoencoders at matched storage. Further analysis shows that SLAE's advantage comes from making wider representations usable under the same storage budget. These gains also extend beyond reconstruction: the information preserved by SLAE improves downstream classification by up to 16.79 percentage points under the same memory budget. Our results suggest a new principle for representation compression: instead of making every latent smaller, keep representations wide and let them share memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperWorld,一个关于文本世界模型中状态序列化结构的受控研究。通过对比原始观察与三种符号序列化方式(独立句子、成对三元组、以实体为中心的hyperedge单元),发现hyperedge序列化在对小模型(0.5B–1.5B)、数据预算有限及分布偏移场景下效果最佳,并在下游贪婪规划中获得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本环境中,语言模型代理需要从序列化的状态描述中学习符号动作效果,但状态序列化结构对学习效果的作用尚未被充分探索。已有工作大多直接使用原始观察或任意序列化方式,缺乏对不同序列化方案的系统比较,因此需要一项受控研究来揭示序列化结构对世界模型学习的影响。

Method: 作者提出HyperWorld,一个受控实验框架:在同一ground-truth状态基础上,比较四种表示——原始观察、独立句子、成对三元组、以实体为中心的hyperedge单元(将实体及其相关事实与关系分组)。所有变体共享相同的训练目标:给定状态和动作,预测符号效果或判断动作不可行。实验覆盖多个模型规模、数据预算、同分布与分布外测试世界,并进行下游贪婪规划评估。

Result: 实验结果显示:hyperedge序列化在0.5B–1.5B模型及分布偏移场景下带来最显著增益;更大模型会缩小差距;成对三元组在分布内精确匹配上与hyperedge持平或略优,但hyperedge在分布外事实F1上最强,且在小到中等规模上实现了可行性检测与效果预测之间的最佳权衡。在下游贪婪规划中,hyperedge世界模型的成功率最高。

Conclusion: 更高阶的状态组织(hyperedge单元)是一种简单但有效的归纳偏置,尤其当模型容量有限或测试环境与训练分布存在差异时。该研究为文本世界模型中状态序列化的设计提供了实证依据,表明表示结构应被视作影响符号世界模型学习的重要因素。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [237] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: I-CARE是一个形式化定义和系统化研究生成式机器遗忘中"干扰"（interference）现象的方法论框架，提供了任务定义、评估指标和结果报告模板，并通过开源工具和Web图形界面加以实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式机器遗忘领域进展迅速，但遗忘过程中对语义相关概念的无意退化（即干扰）现象尚未被充分刻画，且在不同研究中评估方式不一致，缺乏系统性研究框架。

Method: I-CARE不提出新基准或新算法，而是提供一套形式化的方法论，包括：干扰的任务与形式定义、标准化的评估指标、以及结果报告模板。该方法论旨在与具体模型和遗忘算法解耦以保持长期有效性，同时通过当前最优算法和常用数据集进行可行性演示。

Result: 实验表明I-CARE能够在多种遗忘设置下实现对干扰模式的有意义分析，验证了该框架的实际可用性。此外，研究方法已开源，并配备Web图形界面供用户无需编程即可探索实验结果。

Conclusion: I-CARE将干扰确立为生成式遗忘中的一等研究对象，通过统一的形式化定义和评估标准促进该方向的可复现研究与跨设置比较，为领域长期积累科学洞见提供了基础性工具。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [238] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域多物品有容量限制的批量排产问题，其中需求量确定但需求到达时段随机。每个需求在其已知时间窗口内恰好发生一次，且必须不迟于截止日期满足。该问题被建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），并提出遗传算法（GA）在可行状态反馈策略空间中搜索，对330个基准实例的实验表明GA平均最优性差距约3.44%，在90个困难实例上低于5%阈值且平均加速约6.89倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性多物品容量受限批量排产问题忽略了需求到达时段的随机性，而实际生产环境中需求到达时机往往不确定。现有工作难以同时处理需求级别的容量竞争、需求特定的延期交付以及依赖分配的库存动态，因此需要新的随机模型和求解方法。

Method: 将随机问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程，包含状态空间、可行动作、转移核和单期成本函数的完整定义。首先将每个随机实例与对应的确定性实例比较，以隔离随机时序的计算影响。随后提出遗传算法，在可行状态反馈策略空间上搜索，并利用DTMDP转移模型精确评估每个策略。对于无法精确求解的实例，采用经验Bellman时间回归估计缺失的精确求解时间并外推GA加速比。

Result: 330个基准实例的实验显示GA在精确解可用时接近精确随机解，平均最优性差距约3.44%；在90个困难实例中低于5%最优性差距阈值，平均优化加速比为6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。随机时序显著增加了状态数、转移数、求解时间和内存压力。

Conclusion: 所提出的遗传算法能够在随机时序下实现接近最优且显著加速的求解性能，适用于精确求解不可行的困难实例，为随机需求到达时段的批量排产问题提供了实用的近似求解方案。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [239] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于累积轮次的欺诈风险评估框架，通过逐轮聚合对话内容并连续更新风险估计，实现对针对老年人的多轮对话式金融诈骗（投资、慈善、技术支持三类场景）的动态监控。作者构建了含2-8轮对话且在每轮累积阶段都标注风险级别、风险分数、解释理由和安全建议的多轮对话数据集，并在统一训练框架下微调了Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3四个小型语言模型。结果显示Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在参数规模下取得了更强的轮次感知风险估计性能，表明紧凑模型可在移动端和资源受限环境中支持隐私感知的欺诈防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人的金融诈骗越来越多地通过文本和语音渠道（邮件、短信、电话）以多轮对话形式展开，风险信号逐轮渐次浮现，需要能在资源受限部署条件下持续更新风险估计的检测模型，现有方法难以胜任这一动态增量监测需求。

Method: 提出累积轮次风险评估框架：逐步聚合对话轮次并在每一步重新评估风险。构建覆盖投资、慈善和技术支持三类诈骗场景的多轮对话数据集（每条对话2-8轮，每个累积阶段标注定性风险等级、连续风险分数、解释理由及安全建议）。在统一训练框架下微调四个小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1、Qwen3），利用小型模型捕捉欺诈相关语言线索和跨轮升级模式，同时保持紧凑架构以适配移动端和资源受限环境。

Result: 微调后的小型模型能够有效捕捉欺诈语言线索和跨轮升级模式；Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在其参数规模下获得了更强的轮次感知风险估计性能。

Conclusion: 结构化累积建模可支持面向部署场景的增量式诈骗风险评估，同时凸显了紧凑语言模型在隐私感知和端上欺诈防护方面的潜力。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [240] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5加密哈希（196次依赖工具调用、64轮、携带4个32位字）来干净地测试模型在长时程依赖任务中的状态跟踪能力，排除提示理解与工具作弊等干扰因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程LLM评估存在明显缺陷：(1)端到端成功率混淆了状态跟踪困难与指令理解；(2)缺少可隔离状态跟踪难度的对照组；(3)易受幻觉式最终答案等捷径影响，无法定位失败原因。加之缺乏直接证据证明LLM能否在多轮工具调用间精确携带中间状态，因此需要设计一个纯粹测试簿记能力的基准。

Method: 作者让模型逐步执行MD5哈希计算：按RFC 1321从零实现MD5，每条消息生成196次依赖工具调用（每64轮携带a,b,c,d四个32位字）。由于是从头实现，每个调用都能与真实轨迹对齐并逐位校验摘要，因此任何失败都纯属簿记问题。实验使用gpt-oss-120b（MoE，每token约5.5B活跃参数），在温度0下测试。最强设置下将所有原始工具替换为第二个LLM（worker），即driver和worker在无精确算术oracle的情况下从零计算完整哈希。关键变量为：是否将模型自身推理保留在上下文中，以及是否对启用思考的worker进行投票以消除模算术失误。

Result: gpt-oss-120b在简短固定提示、温度0的设置下，能在多数完成的运行中携带全部状态穿过196次调用并返回正确摘要。在最强设置（driver+worker双LLM计算）中，两个关键因素（但与权重无关）决定成败：每轮将模型自身推理保留在上下文中，以及对思考型worker进行投票以消除模算术错误。失败可按来源分类定位为状态携带、算术和服务问题。

Conclusion: LLM确实可以在很长序列的依赖工具调用间精确携带中间状态（至少在MD5这类纯簿记任务上，多数运行成功），且成败可由上下文管理和投票等非权重策略决定。残差失败可按状态携带、算术和服务器原因分别定位。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [241] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenAgentFlow，一个控制面/行动面（control-plane/action-plane）架构，用于在异构AI智能体（多智能体、规划器、控制器及执行后端）环境中，于行动提交边界处强制执行安全策略。它将GUI操作、API调用、工具调用及LLM生成调用统一标准化为AgentEvent流，通过共享的前置执行策略强制点（PEP）进行路由，并在控制面维护溯源、会话状态、审计记录和可更新的策略。在Android上的300例事件基准中达到94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；在30例动态策略套件中27例匹配预期行为；在98例Android模拟器追踪案例中达到90.8%原始准确率和92.9%追踪调整通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的AI智能体正从孤立助手演化为异构系统，多个主体在同一环境中并发操作，安全问题已上升为系统级行动治理问题——即决定是否在智能体生成的行动修改共享状态之前提交这些行动。现有安全防护覆盖提示词、工具调用、GUI操作和智能体本地行为，但存在执法碎片化、难以揭示跨多步骤行动流中涌现的风险、以及对可审计性和策略演进支持不足等问题。

Method: OpenAgentFlow采用控制面与行动面分离的架构：在行动面将待执行的GUI操作、API调用、工具调用及LLM生成调用统一规范化为AgentEvent流；每个事件经过共享的前置执行策略强制点（Policy Enforcement Point）进行路由检查；控制面维护溯源信息、会话状态、审计记录及可更新策略。该架构使新规则无需修改智能体、提示词、模型或执行路径即可生效。

Result: 在Android上实例化OpenAgentFlow：300例行动事件基准达到94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；30例动态策略套件中安装新规则后27例匹配预期行为；98例Android模拟器追踪案例中（来自100例套件）达到90.8%原始准确率和92.9%追踪调整通过率，覆盖GUI、API及LLM规划的案例类型。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明OpenAgentFlow为异构AI智能体集群提供了一个实用的共享强制执行边界，能够统一治理多种类型的智能体行动，支持动态策略更新，并保持良好的准确率与攻击拦截能力。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [242] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCAFFOLD，一个大规模计算机科学论文图表数据集，包含图像、标题、上下文、问答和思维链推理，分别提供157K/37K/12K三种规模版本，并在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct上进行基线实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文高度依赖图表（架构图、系统流程图、管道示意图等），这些图表往往承载比文字更多的信息，但目前缺乏将这类图形与标题、上下文、问题和分步推理相结合的公开数据集来训练视觉语言模型。

Method: 从arXiv计算机科学论文中，利用布局检测和PDF解析（PDF parsing）准备数据，配合AI辅助问题生成步骤，构建(图像、标题、上下文、问答、思维链)元组的结构化数据集SCAFFOLD，并划分为SCAFFOLD-157K（3,058篇论文，29,887张图，157,387对）、SCAFFOLD-37K（36,797对）和SCAFFOLD-12K（12,000对）三个规模。

Result: 发布了SCAFFOLD数据集，覆盖3,058篇论文共29,887张图表（157,387对样本），以及中等规模（37K对）和小规模（12K对）版本；使用Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct在SCAFFOLD-12K上进行了基线实验。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD提供了首个配对计算机科学论文图表与问答及思维链推理的大规模公开数据集，为训练视觉语言模型理解CS研究图表奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [243] [UI-Venus-2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00028)
*Venus Team,Zhuohan Cai,Haoxing Chen,Jiaxuan Chen,Weizhi Chen,Changlong Gao,Zhangxuan Gu,Yuan Guo,Yusong Hu,Jianrong Jiang,Jianguo Li,Runze Li,Jinzhen Lin,Zhenyu Ma,Changhua Meng,Han Peng,Xinyu Qiu,Shuheng Shen,Zhongyi Shui,Weiqiang Wang,Ming Wen,Zhuoer Xu,Hang Yan,Kaiwen Yang,Ruilin Yao,Nanjun Yu,Zhengwen Zeng,Lianrui Zhang,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhe Zhao,Beitong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UI-Venus-2，一个跨移动、网页和桌面环境的多模态GUI基础智能体，通过统一闭环推理-行动框架，从环境覆盖、任务构建和验证机制三个维度的扩展，以及安全意识的集成，推动GUI智能体从基准导向向可靠现实应用部署的转型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态GUI智能体虽已成为数字任务自动化的有前景范式，但从基准导向模型过渡到可靠的现实世界应用仍面临挑战，具体表现为环境覆盖有限、任务构建脆弱以及奖励验证不可靠。

Method: (1) 环境维度：扩展到170多个多语言移动应用和原生桌面操作系统；(2) 任务维度：采用深度研究管线进行基于功能的指令生成；(3) 验证维度：采用轨迹级和样本级评估器，结合视觉关键点和多模型投票，为RL训练提供可靠的奖励信号。此外集成安全感知机制以确保关键操作的可控执行。

Result: UI-Venus-2作为一个具备能力、高效且开源的通用基础GUI智能体，通过三方面联合扩展和内置安全机制，实现了跨环境的统一闭环推理-行动范式，推动领域向更通用、可验证和自反思的智能体方向发展。

Conclusion: UI-Venus-2通过联合扩展环境、任务和验证三个关键维度，并提供开源基础，为现实世界中更可泛化、可验证且具备自我反思能力的GUI智能体奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for digital task automation, yet transitioning from benchmark-oriented models to dependable real-world applications remains challenging due to limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward verification. In this work, we present UI-Venus-2, a general-purpose foundation GUI agent designed to operate across mobile, web, and desktop environments through a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. To bridge the gap toward practical deployment, we jointly scale three critical dimensions: (1) Environments, expanding coverage to more than 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop operating systems; (2) Tasks, employing a deep-research pipeline for function-grounded instruction generation; and (3) Verification, adopting trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting to ensure reliable RL signals for training. Furthermore, we integrate safety-aware mechanisms to ensure controlled execution of consequential actions. By offering a capable, efficient, and open-source foundation, UI-Venus-2 advances the field toward more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents for real-world applications.

</details>


### [244] [EULER: Exploring Underused Links with Evidence-Checked Return for Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00032)
*Ren Zhenzhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EULER是一个以"桥梁"（问题跨数学领域迁移）为搜索单元的多智能体系统。该系统围绕固定猜想，同时运行直接、相邻领域和远距离领域三条搜索路线；桥梁只有在提供源表示无法执行的操作、且目标侧证据能沿已验证的蕴涵链返回原命题时才保留预算。六项有序压力测试在昂贵搜索开始前拒绝无效桥梁。在120个近期猜想（冻结于搜索前并经过污染筛查，来自JCTA期刊作者论文）上，EULER产出10个证明、3个反例和45个有范围的局部结果。消融实验表明桥梁特定压力测试将错误结论从9降至3，桥梁材料与目标原生操作结合产生+4.2的正面交互。领域距离并非成功可靠预测因子，可执行的操作增益和有效返回才是。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学社区使用不同的对象、不变量和工具工作，跨领域转移问题代价高昂且常常被跳过。作者希望构建一个系统，以"桥梁"（即跨领域问题转移）为基本搜索单元，系统化地利用跨领域迁移来推进猜想求解。

Method: EULER采用多智能体架构，围绕固定猜想并行运行三条路线：直接（源领域内）、相邻领域和远距离领域。每条路线产生的"桥梁"须满足两个条件才保留预算：(1) 提供源表示无法执行的操作；(2) 目标侧证据能沿一条被检查过的蕴涵链返回到原始命题。系统设计六项有序压力测试，在昂贵搜索前拒绝无效桥梁以节省计算资源。评估采用120个冻结在搜索前、经污染筛查、来自JCTA（组合学权威期刊）近期作者论文中的猜想。

Result: EULER产出10个证明、3个反例和45个有范围的局部结果。消融实验中，桥梁特定压力测试将错误结论数量从9减至3；桥梁材料与目标原生操作组合产生了+4.2个解决任务的正面交互效应（单独任一因素都无法实现）。领域距离不能可靠预测成功率，而起决定作用的是可执行操作增益和有效返回。

Conclusion: 跨领域迁移（桥梁）可以成为自动化数学发现的有效搜索单元。关键的预测因子不是领域距离本身，而是桥梁是否带来源领域无法执行的操作增益，以及证据能否有效返回原始命题。受控的压力测试对于降低错误率至关重要。

Abstract: Mathematical communities work with different objects, invariants, and tools, so transferring a problem across them is expensive and often skipped. We present EULER, a multi-agent system that takes such a transfer--a bridge--as its unit of search. Around a fixed conjecture, EULER runs direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes in competition; a bridge keeps its budget only if it supplies an operation the source representation cannot execute and its target-side evidence returns to the original statement along a checked implication. Six ordered stress tests reject invalid bridges before expensive search begins.
  We evaluate EULER on 120 recent conjectures. The conjectures were frozen before search and screened for contamination, and are drawn from public papers by authors who had recently published in the Journal of Combinatorial Theory, Series A, a leading journal in combinatorics. EULER produced 10 proofs and 3 refutations, plus 45 scoped partial results. Two mechanisms held up under ablation: bridge-specific stress tests cut incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3, and bridge material combined with a target-native operation yielded a positive interaction of +4.2 resolved tasks that neither factor produced alone. Domain distance did not reliably predict success; executable operation gain and valid return did.

</details>


### [245] [When Prediction Error Is Not Enough: Evaluating Nuisance-Function Prediction for Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00071)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过蒙特卡洛模拟，在部分线性模型背景下比较了OLS、GAMs、XGBoost和DML-XGBoost等估计器的因果推断性能，发现预测误差不能直接作为因果估计器质量的衡量标准，预测误差与因果偏差之间的关系在不同方法和场景中并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果推断中，预测误差常被用于评估nuisance函数估计器，但其与因果估计器性能之间的关系可能因性能指标而异，现有研究中对此缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 在部分线性模型下进行蒙特卡洛模拟，比较OLS、GAMs、XGBoost和DML-XGBoost四种方法，评估nuisance函数预测误差、偏差、RMSE及95%置信区间覆盖率，并引入基于暴露和结局nuisance函数估计误差绝对交叉乘积的联合误差指标。

Result: XGBoost在非oracle方法中RMSE最低；DML-XGBoost在置信区间覆盖率方面表现更佳。预测误差在不同方法和场景下并未一致地反映因果偏差，点估计性能最好的方法不一定有最好的置信区间覆盖率。联合误差指标与因果偏差仅有弱关联，不能作为独立的因果性能衡量标准。

Conclusion: 预测误差有助于评估nuisance函数的估计质量，但不应被视为因果估计器质量的直接度量标准。

Abstract: Prediction error is widely used to evaluate nuisance-function estimators in causal inference, but its relationship with causal estimator performance may differ across performance measures. We studied this question in a partially linear model using Monte Carlo simulations. We compared ordinary least squares (OLS), generalized additive models (GAMs), XGBoost, and Double Machine Learning with XGBoost (DML-XGBoost), evaluating nuisance-function prediction error, bias, RMSE, and 95\% confidence interval coverage. We also examined a simple joint-error measure based on the absolute cross-product of estimation errors from the exposure and outcome nuisance functions. Across the simulated settings, XGBoost had the lowest RMSE among the non-oracle methods, while DML-XGBoost generally provided better confidence interval coverage. Prediction error did not consistently track causal bias across methods and settings, and the method with the best point-estimation performance did not necessarily have the best confidence interval coverage. The joint-error measure was only weakly associated with causal bias and did not provide a useful standalone measure of causal performance. These results suggest that prediction error is useful for assessing nuisance-function estimation, but it should not be treated as a direct measure of the quality of the resulting causal estimator.

</details>


### [246] [MiNER: Fine-Tuned Biomedical Natural Language Processing for Malaria Disease Entity Recognition in Clinical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00073)
*V. S. Anoop,Devika N*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微调BioBERT的预训练生物医学语言模型方法，用于从疟疾科学文献中提取生物医学信息（命名实体和关系）。方法首先选择和预处理大规模疟疾文献语料并标注临床相关实体，然后利用BioBERT编码文本并基于领域标注进行监督微调。实验表明该方法在精确率、召回率和准确率上显著优于其他编码和机器学习算法，并发布了人工标注数据集供其他研究者使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾仍是全球重大健康负担，需要不断研究其分子机制、流行病学和治疗方法。从庞大且不断增长的疟疾文献中提取关键生物医学信息极具挑战性，亟需创新的自动化方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 该方法包含四个关键步骤：(1) 选择并预处理大规模疟疾科学文章语料；(2) 对语料进行具有临床意义的命名实体注释；(3) 利用BioBERT（先进的预训练语言模型）将文本编码为上下文感知表示；(4) 使用领域特定标注和监督学习对模型进行微调，以增强其生物医学命名实体提取能力。

Result: 大量实验以及与不同编码和机器学习算法的对比表明，所提方法在精确率、召回率和准确率等指标上均显著优于对比方法。此外，作者还发布了人工标注的实体和关系提取数据集。

Conclusion: 微调生物医学预训练语言模型（BioBERT）能够有效提升疟疾文献中的生物医学信息提取性能，发布的数据集也将为其他健康信息学研究者训练先进疟疾信息提取模型提供支持。

Abstract: Malaria remains a significant global health burden, necessitating continuous research efforts to understand its complex molecular mechanisms, epidemiology, and potential therapeutic interventions. Extracting essential biomedical information from the vast and constantly growing malaria literature is a challenging task that demands innovative approaches. Recently, pre-trained language models have revolutionized natural language processing tasks, demonstrating remarkable capabilities in various domains. This paper proposes a fine-tuned pre-trained biomedical language model for biomedical information extraction from scientific literature on malaria disease.
